   	ceṉṉi - 600 006,  maṟṟum pirācacu intiyā, 
tiruvallikkēṇi - 600 005.
yāṉ aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka ērāḷamāṉa nūlkaḷil ārāyntu kaṇṭa muṭivukaḷi nūlkaḷāka eḻutiyirukkiṉṟēṉ.
pāṟkaṭalik kaṭintu amutam eṭuttatu pōla, uṇmi kāṇum uṇarcciyiṉāl naṭunili niṉṟu ārāyn tirukkiṉṟēṉ. 
oruvar tam vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum kaṟṟālum aṟiyamuṭiyāta uṇmikaḷi ellām eḷitāka eḻuti vittirukkiṉṟēṉ.
ellārum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ yāvaṟṟiyum kaṟkum tolliyi mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya tilli, atu muṭiyavum muṭiyātu, tēviyumilli. 
ellā nūlkaḷilum uḷḷa ciṟanta uṇmikaḷi ellām piḻi cāṟāka yāṉ vaṭittut tantuḷḷēṉ.
eṉ nūlkaḷip paṭittāṟpōtum, ataṉāl, tamiḻ muḻutuṅ kaṟṟa payaṉi aṭiyalām.
- maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ



cāṉṟitaḻ
nāki - tiru. vētācalam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ orupōtu tiruvaṉantapurattil oruvāran taṅkiyirunta ñāṉṟu, avaruṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻakum vāyppuk kiṭittataṟkuc cālavum makiḻkiṉṟēm. varuṅkālat tamiḻ makkaḷukku maṭṭaṟṟa tamiḻppulami mikuntu varumeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka ivar iḷamiyilēyē nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulam uṭiyarāyiruppatu kaḷippūṭṭukiṟatu. maṟṟum tāymoḻip paṟṟuk kuṟinta ikkālattil, ivar tamatu nuṇṇaṟivit tamiḻ nūlkaḷil muṟṟum celutti naṉkārāyntuḷḷatum pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu. pattuppāṭṭu, kalittoki, cīvakacintāmaṇi mutaliya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil ārāycciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya ivartam aṟivuṭimiyē eṉṉi ivarpāl matippuṟumāṟu ceytatu. tamiḻāciriyarkaḷiṉ toki curuṅkivarum ikkālattil ivarukkuc ciṟanta ūkkamaḷittāl ivar oru ciṟanta tamiḻāciriyarāvāreṉṟu nām uṟutiyāka nampukiṉṟēm. muṉṉarē iraṇṭāntarak kallūrikaḷil tamiḻkaṟpikkap pōtumāṉa aḷavu ilakkaṇa ilakkiyattiṟ ciṟanta tērcci peṟṟavarāyirukkiṉṟāreṉak karutukiṉṟōm. Fiwªj msÉš v~¥, V., (F.A.) vakuppukkuriya āṅkila aṟivu peṟṟārāṉāl mēlnāṭṭu muṟippaṭi tamiḻ nūlkaḷi varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟuṭaṉ ārāyavum eḻutavum vallunarāvāreṉpatu emmuṭiya ciṟanta eṇṇam.

vaṇṇārappēṭṭi	maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntarampiḷḷi
tirunelvēli	tamiḻttāy vāḻttiṉ  āciriyar
2-12-1895
(inta naṟcāṉṟu tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil cuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ pērāciriyarāka iruntapōtu koṭukkap peṟṟatu.)

maṟinta aṭikaḷār! tamiḻ ñāyiṟu
āṅkilam, āriyam, tīntamiḻ - eṉṟa mūṉṟiyum tuṟipōka muṟṟakkaṟṟa oru mūtaṟiñar entam aṭikaḷ. mummoḻikaḷiyum orucēra ivvāṟu tamiḻulakil mācaṟak kaṟṟa nallāciriyar ivarkku muṉṉumilli; ivarkkup piṉṉu milli. itu tamiḻ varalāṟu.
vaṭamoḻiyum tamiḻum vēṟu vēṟu eṉpati viḷakkik kāṭṭiṉār. āciriyar civañāṉamuṉivar. (18ām nūṟ.) vaṭa moḻiyi aṟavē akaṟṟiviṭṭut tamiḻtaṉittu iyaṅkavallatu eṉpati ārāyntu coṉṉār ṭākṭar kālṭuvel (19ām nūṟ.) avvāṟu pallāyiram ēṭukaḷ tamiḻil eḻutik kāṭṭiṉār āciriyar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ. (20ām nūṟ.) moḻi varalāṟṟil inta mūṉṟum mūṉṟu māṇikka uṇmikaḷ.
aṭikaḷār ēṟṟiya viḷakku iṉṉum erikiṉṟatu cāṉṟōrē! emmi maṟantālum yām maṟakka māṭṭēmāl, nīvīr aṭivittu naṭanta avvaḻiyi ematu neñcu nāṭukiṟatu. aṭikaḷ maṟinta attici nōkki entali tāḻvatāka!
- vittuvāṉ a. kiruṣṇamūrtti 
               (pak. 48)

maṟimaliya māṇpu
nuḻivuri
 taṉittamiḻ eṉṟa aḷavil muṉ niṟkum muḻuturu eḻilvaṭivar
 tavattiru. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārē āvar!
munti aṭikaḷ ākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, `muttamiḻ aṭikaḷ eṉa muḻakkamiṭa niṉṟār!
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷō, taṉittamiḻt tanti eṉṉum takavārnta perumi peṟṟār.
tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyatu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻit tuṇiyiṉṟi iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, ulaka moḻikaḷuk kellām tāymoḻi vaṭamoḻiyē eṉṟum  tamiḻk kalvi valla peṟṟiyarum muṟṟum mayaṅki yurikka,
vaṭamoḻi ulaka moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻi eṉṟāl, ataṟkum maṟṟi moḻikaḷukkum tanti moḻi tamiḻē eṉṟu kūṟiya vaṭalūr aṭikaḷār pukaḻpparapāḷarāyp potunilik kaḻakam kaṇṭa tava aṭikaḷār, taṉittamiḻt tanti eṉṉum perumi peṟṟa maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār āvar.
civaṉiyam cārnta perumakkaḷum, pēraṟivarkaḷum kūṭa, oppārāy, `taṉittamiḻ (taṉittu amiḻ) eṉṟu paḻikkavum iḻikkavum, tuṇintu niṉṟu, avar veḷka; veḷḷiṭi maliyākat `taṉittamiḻ eṉpati nili nāṭṭiya niṟimali, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārē!
civaṉiya maṭattiṉarum, vaṭamoḻi vallārum, `tol kāppiyak  kaṭal eṉap pukaḻappaṭṭārumām cāmināta tēcikar, nāṇamiṉṟi, 
  ainteḻuttāl orupāṭi eṉṟu aṟiyavē nāṇuvar
  aṟivuṭiyōrē; yāmum atuvē
eṉṟum,
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭō?
eṉṟum - eḻutip parappiṉārāka,
oru col tāṉum vēṟṟuc col viravā nūl yām ceyvēm eṉṟu aimpāṉ nāṉku nūlkaḷ kalimali pēḻiyāy eḻutik kāṭcip paṭuttiya māṭciyar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār!
tolkāppiyak kāval
tamiḻ moḻikku vāytta tolkāppiya vaḷanūl,
vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
  eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē	(884)
eṉṟum,
moḻi peyarppuc ceytalilum muṟimi pēṇa vēṇṭum eṉpati,
moḻipeyarttu atarppaṭa yāttal	(1597)
eṉṟum,
pēccu vaḻakkil oru kāl vēṟṟuccol vantu viṭiṉum,
marapunili tirital ceyyuṭ killi 	(1590)
eṉṟum - varampāṇi ceytum, ac cevviyal vaḻittaṭam citintum cīraḻintum, cēṟum cakatiyum nāṟum ciṟumiyum kuṟumiyum vaḻivaḻiyē eyta mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷāy, ayalōr cūḻcciyālum tiṟavōr mayakkālum ēṟpaṭṭa aḻipāṭukaḷi ellām tāmē ōr iyakkamāy iruntu, viḻippum eḻucciyum ūṭṭiyavar maṟimali aṭikaḷē eṉpatāl,
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻi mīṭṭeṭuppa taṟkāka arum pāṭupaṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa marapōṭu yāṉ kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu eṉṉum pāvāṇar,
mūvā yiramāṇṭu mōtum vaṭamoḻiyāl
 cāvām takiniṉṟa taṇṭamiḻi - mēvāk
 kumarit taṉinilikkuk koṇṭuvanta  appar
 amarar maṟimali yār
eṉap pāṭip paraviṉār. `taṉit tamiḻt tantiyār eṉṟa pārāṭṭum ip pāṭṭiṉ talippil vaḻaṅkiṉār.
nuṇṇiya nūlpalavum kaṟpatē nōṉpāka
 eṇṇiya vāḻnāḷ elāmaḷittup - paṇṇiya
 centamiḻnūl ovvoṉṟum ceppum kaṭalaṟivi
 pintamiḻ kākkum paṭi
eṉa aṭikaḷ kalvi mēmpāṭṭiyum kalit tiṟap paṭippukaḷiṉ mēmpāṭṭiyum pāvēntar pāratitācaṉār pārāṭṭukiṟār.
paltuṟi āṟṟal
aṭikaḷiṉ paltuṟi āṟṟali 1. pērāciriyar, 2. perum pulavar, 3. pāvalar, 4. ārāycciyāḷar, 5. mummoḻip pulavar, 6. moḻipeyarppāḷar, 7. coṟpoḻi vāḷar, 8. eḻuttāḷar, 9. palkalic celvar, 10. taṉittamiḻt tantiyār eṉṟu pāvāṇar pakuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār.
mēlum avar tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulatti
paṉimali mukaṭṭiṉ uyarvum, nīla āṟṟiṉ nīḷamum amiti vāriyiṉ āḻamum amintavar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉkiṟār. (paṉimali - imayam; nīla āṟu - nil; amitivāri - pacipikku mākaṭal).
aṭikaḷār nūlkaḷ
aṭikaḷ paṉmāṇ māttamiḻ nūlkaḷi, nūlaka iyakkat tanti araṅka nātarum, innāḷ civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻaka āṭciyarum annāḷ nūlakarumākiya tirumiku irā. muttuk kumāracāmi avarkaḷum 14 vakimippaṭutti vittami perum pārāṭṭukku uriyatām avi,
1. maruttuvam  2. maṟiporuḷ  3. ilakkiyam 4. itaḻkaḷ  5. caṅka  ilakkiyam  6. pāṭal 7. nāṭakam  8. putiṉam 9. kaṭitam 10. kaṭṭuri 11. camayam  12. tattuvam 13. varalāṟu 14. camūka iyal eṉpaṉa.
mutaṟ kuṟaḷ vāta nirākaraṇam eṉṉum maṟuppu nūlē, aṭikaḷiṉ mutal nūlāka veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu (1898) annūṉ maṟuppu nayamē nāki aṭikaḷi - pallavapura aṭikaḷāka -taṉittamiḻ aṭikaḷāka ākka arum perum tūṇṭal āyiṟṟām.
aṭikaḷār varalāṟu: kīḻkaṭal cār nākappaṭṭiṉattic cārnta ciṟṟūr kāṭampāṭi, āṅku vāḻnta aruntavap peruntakiyar cokkanātar - ciṉṉammiyār! ivviṇiyar koṭiyāyt tamiḻaṉṉi ceyta tavap pēṟṟāl piṟantavar vētācalam. piṟanta peru maṅkala nāḷ ki.pi. 15.07.1876.
cokkanātar, cittamalam aṟuvitta cittamaruttuvar; aṟiva maruttuvam eṉpatum atu. attoḻil avarkku vaḷatti vaḻaṅkiyatu. ataṉāl vētācalam kuṟivaṟa vāḻntār.
nākappaṭṭiṉattu veciliyaṉ micaṉ paḷḷiyil aintu vakuppuvari payiṉṟār; ciṟanta māṇavarāka viḷaṅkiṉār. āṟām vakuppil cērum niliyil, arumit tantiyār iyaṟki eytiṉār. āyiṉum aṉṉiyār uṟutiyāl kalviyit toṭarntār. appaḷḷiyil āṅkilak kalvikku irunta mutaṉmi tamiḻukku illi. kiṟittava camayam paṟṟi aṉṟic civaṉiyam paṟṟi aṟiya vāyppilli eṉṉum iraṇṭu kuṟikaḷiyum iḷamiyilēyē uṇarntār.
vīṭṭilēyē tamiḻ nūlkaḷip payiṉṟu mutaṟ kuṟiyiyum, nākiyil irunta intumatāpimāṉa caṅkam vaḻi iraṇṭām kuṟiyiyum nīkkiṉār. paḷḷiyilum kuṟiyaṟak kaṟṟār. varuvāyk kuṟiyāl vāṭum aṉṉikkuc cumiyāy illāmal taṉiyē payilvatē iṉiyavaḻi eṉa eṇṇi, nākiyil puttaka vaṇikarāka viḷaṅkiya nārāyaṇa cāmiyār pulamiyālum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyavar. ātalāl avariṭam taṉiyē ilakkiya  - ilakkaṇa - camaya nūlkaḷik kaṟṟār. nārāyaṇariṉ muṉṉi māṇavar pērā. maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntaraṉār eṉpatiyum aṟintār. oṉpatām vakuppoṭu paḷḷik kalviyi niṟuttit taṉikkalviyē kaṟṟār. tolkāppiyam, toki nūlkaḷ, tirukkuṟaḷ, tirucciṟṟampalak kōvi, iṟiyaṉār akap poruḷ eṉakkaṟṟār.
aṭikaḷār iyaṟṟiya tiruvoṟṟi murukar mummaṇik kōvi mukappil, ematu patiṉintām āṇṭil muṟippaṭi tuvaṅkiya tamiḻp payiṟciyāṉatu ematu irupattōrām āṇṭiṟkuḷ tolkāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ, ciṟṟampalak kōviyār eṉṉum mūṉṟu nūlkaḷiyum muḻutum neṭṭuruc ceytu muṭittēm eṉṟum vēṟucila nūlkaḷiyum muḻuvatāka ōtiyatiyum kūṟi,
iṅṅaṉamāka, viḻumiya tamiḻppaḻa nūlkaḷil ematu karuttu īrppuṇṭu niṉṟu payiṉṟa payiṟciyiṉālēyē ceyyuḷum uriyum taṉic centamiḻ naṭiyil eḻutum tiṟam ematu iḷamip paruvattilēyē emakku vāyppa tāyiṟṟu eṉkiṟār. avvāṇṭu ki.pi. 1897. nāki intu matāpimāṉa caṅkattiṟkuc civa cittānta caṇṭa marutam eṉṉum virutu peṟṟa cōmacuntara nāyakar vantu coṟpoḻivāṟṟiṉār. avar poḻivil vanta karuttoṉṟaṉi maṟuttu oruvar eḻuta, ataṟku maṟuppāka murukavēḷ eṉṉum puṉipeyaril maṟuppuri eḻutiṉār. ataṉikkaṟṟa nāyakar, mīḷap poḻivukku varuṅkāl murukavēḷic cantikka virumpiṉār. accantippiṉ payaṉē uṉṉi virivilē ceṉṉikku varuvippōm nī antap pakkaṅkaḷil iruppatē nallatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. ituvē aṭikaḷārkkuc ceṉṉi vāḻvu amiya mutal tūṇṭal āyiṟṟu. aṭikaḷukkup patiṉēḻām vayatil (1893) tirumaṇam nikaḻntatu. tuṇivi tam māmaṉ makaḷ cavuntara valli eṉpār.
tiruvaṉantapuram
nārāyaṇacāmi avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉi māṇavarām pērā. cuntaraṉār tiruvaṉanta purattil iruntār. avariyaṟṟiya maṉōṉmaṇiya nāṭaka nūl kaṟṟu ataṉ arumi kaṇṭu, pērāciriyarik kāṇavirumpiṉār. ataṉāl tam āciriyarōṭu tiruvaṉantapuram ceṉṟu pērāciriyarōṭu uriyāṭiṉār. vētācalaṉār iḷam paruvattiyum mūtaṟiviyum aṟinta cuntaraṉār viyappuṟṟut takutic cāṉṟu oṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉār. ciṟitu kālam tiruvaṉanta purattil āciriyap paṇiyum ceytār. avviṭattiṉ paruvanili ovvāmiyāl nākikkē tirumpiṉār.
ceṉṉi: cōmacuntaraṉār muṉṉē kūṟiyapaṭi vētācalaric ceṉṉikku aḻittu aṟiñar nallucāmi eṉpār naṭāttiya cittānta tīpiki eṉṉum itaḻāciriyar ākkiṉār. piṉṉē ceṉṉi kiṟittavak kallūrikku oru tamiḻp pērāciriyar vēṇṭiyiruttal aṟinta cōmacuntaraṉār vētācalaṉā ra akkallūrip pērāciriyarākka muyaṉṟār. atukāl tamiḻttuṟit talivarāka iruntavar vi.kō cūriyanārāyaṇa cāttiriyār eṉṉum paritimāl kaliñar. kallūri mutalvar villiyam millar eṉpār.
9.3.1898 il vētācalaṉār paṇiyēṟṟār. aṅkup patiṉmūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ paṇiyāṟṟiyuḷḷār.
akkallūrik kālattē iḷaṅkali māṇavarkkup pāṭamāka irunta mullippāṭṭu (1903) paṭṭiṉappāli (1906) eṉpavaṟṟukku ārāycci uri eḻutiṉār.
avvuri nūli vāṅkik kaṟṟa māṇākkarum ēṉit tamiḻaṟiñarum putumuṟiyāka eḻutappaṭṭa avvurip pāṅkiṉip piṉpaṟṟip paḻiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷi ārāyvatil kiḷarcci peṟalāṉār. ipputumuṟi uri, tamiḻ nūlārāyum vakikaḷi iṉitu viḷakki aṟiñar palarkkum aka makiḻcci yiṉi viḷittami kaṇṭu iṉi, ittakiya urikaḷiyē eḻututal vēṇṭum eṉa ematuḷḷam peritum viḻintatu eṉkiṟār.
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattārāṅkilamum vaṭa moḻiyum kaṭṭāyamākap paṭittal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tāymoḻip pāṭatti viruppap pāṭamākak kaṟpikkalām eṉṟum oru tīrmāṉam āṭcikkuḻuvil koṇṭu vantaṉar. oru pāṭam viruppappāṭam eṉappaṭṭa piṉṉar ataṉik kaṟka varuvār evar? tamiḻ kaṟkum māṇavar eṇṇikki kuṟiyavē āciriyap paṇiyiṭamum kuṟiya vēṇṭuvatāki, aṭikaḷār 31.1. 1911 ām nāḷuṭaṉ kallūrip paṇiyi muṭikka vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.
māṇavar: ikkālattil aṭikaḷiṉ māṇavarāka iruntāruḷ, taṇikimaṇi ceṅkalvarāyar, iracikamaṇi ṭi.kē.ci, nāvalar ca.cō. pārati, pēraṟiñar ca. viyāpuriyār mutalōr cilar.
taṇikimaṇiyiṉ, tēvāra tiruvācakac collaṭivu oru piṟappaḷavil ceyyak kūṭiyatā?
nāvalar ca. cō. pāratiyāriṉ āyvut tuṇivu ettaṉi kacaṭukaḷi nīkkiyatu? viyāpuriyār, kāla āyvilum, vaṭamoḻip piṭivātattilum tammic cālpu āyvil iruntu vilakkiṉālum, caṅka ilakkiyap patippu, nikaṇṭuppatippu, palkalik kaḻaka akaramutalip patippu ivaṟṟic ceyta arumi eṉṉa? puṟattiraṭṭu, kaḷaviyal, kāriki āyavaṟṟiṉ patippuc ciṟappu eṉṉa?
ivarkaḷ paṇiyum aṭikaḷārit taḻuvutal uṇmi tāṉē!
camaraca caṉmārkkam
kallūrip paṇiyil iruntu vīṭupeṟṟa aṭikaḷār, pallavapuratti vāḻviṭamākak koṇṭār. camaraca caṉmārkka niliyam tōṟṟuvittār. 
ellāc cātiyārkkum ellāc camayattārkkum aṅkīkāramāṉa cīvakāruṇiya oḻukkattiyum, camaraca caṉmārk kattiyum piṟkālattil mika valiyuṟuttip pōtitta āciriyar srīmat irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷē āvār. accuvāmikaḷ valiyuṟuttiya avviraṇṭu koḷkikaḷiyum eṅkum valiyuṟutti viḷakki aṉparkaḷi oruṅku kūṭṭa vēṇṭi accuvāmikaḷ iṭṭa peyarālēyē, `camaraca caṉmārkka niliyam eṉṉum itu tāpikkalāyiṟṟu. inniliyattiṟku muṉ āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār; piṉ āciriyar irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷ āvar eṉṟu peyarīṭu ceytu koḷkikaḷiyum vakukkiṟār.
kuṭumpam
1898il aṭikaḷār ceṉṉikku varumpōtu avartam aṉṉiyār ciṉṉammiyār, tuṇiviyār cavuntiram ammi yār, celvi cintāmaṇi, aṭikaḷār eṉṉum nālvarik koṇṭa kuṭumpamāka iruntatu.
1911il pallava purattil kuṭipukum pōtu nīlāmpiki, tiruñāṉa campantam, māṇikkavācakam, tirunāvukkaracu, cuntara mūrtti eṉṉum makkaḷum koṇṭa oṉpatiṉmar kuṭumpamāka iruntatu. pallavuram pukunta piṉ tiripura cuntari piṟakkap patiṉmaṉ kuṭumpam āyiṟṟu. inniliyilēyē 27.08.1911il civat tuṟaviyarkkuriya tuṟavāṭi (kāvi) koṇṭu cuvāmi vētācalam āṉār. cuvāmi vētācalam eṉpataṉ taṉittamiḻākkamē maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. muṉṉēyē avari aṭikaḷ eṉṟatu avartam māṇpu karutiyēyām.
mīḷpārvi
nām mīḷpārvi oṉṟupārkka vēṇṭum.
aṭikaḷ 21ām akaviyilēyē taṉittamiḻ āyvuttiṟam kivarap peṟṟār.
1903, 1907 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil mullippāṭṭu, paṭṭiṉappāli ākiya nūlkaḷukku āyvuri vaḻaṅkuṅkāl itiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ ittaṉi; ivaṟṟuḷ centamiḻccol ittaṉi; vēṟṟuc col ittaṉi - eṉa aṟutiyiṭukiṟār.
ātalāl, taṉittamiḻ eṉṉum tōṟṟam 1916 ām āṇṭil nīlāmpikiyār tūṇṭalāl ēṟpaṭṭami eṉpatu, akattē amintu kiṭanta vittu, muḷi viṭṭu veḷippaṭṭa niliyām. av veḷippaṭṭa niliyum uṭaṉē paṟṟip paṭaravilli! mēlum cila āṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉṉarēyē camacara caṉmārkka niliyam, potunilik kaḻakamākavum, ñāṉacākaram aṟivuk kaṭalākavum, cuvāmi vētācalam maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ ākavum paṭippaṭiyē kiḷarkiṉṟaṉavām!
ōr ariya amippu uruvākum niliyum, ōr ariya kaṇṭu piṭippu ulakavar aṟiya veḷippaṭalum, avi iyakkamākavum payaṉākavum ākiṉṟa niliyum paṟṟiya viḷakkam, aṭikaḷār taṉittamiḻ iyakka varalāṟṟil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatām!
etiriṭit tūṇṭal: mēlum oṉṟu: oru nēriṭit tūṇṭaliṉum etiriṭit tūṇṭal valimi mikkatu eṉpatum, uḷḷattu uṟutiyi veḷḷap perukkāy veḷippaṭutta vallatu eṉpatum teḷivām.
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭā? eṉṟu kaṟṟaṟinta tamiḻar oruvarē viṉāvi nakittal, peṟṟa tāy moḻip piṟavikku uṟṟa takimi ākātu eṉpatum, ataṉi uṇarnta appiṟavi pāmpaṉ kurupāta aṭikaḷ pōl cēntaṉ centamiḻ eṉṉa oru nūliyaṟṟit tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkavalla taṉi uyar moḻi eṉṟu nilināṭṭiyiruppiṉ vaḻipaṭattakka piṟaviyāy amintirukkum! appēṟu ataṟku vāykkāmi,
 tuṟantārkkut tuppuravu vēṇṭi maṟantārkol 
  maṟṟi yavarkaḷ tavam	(kuṟaḷ 263)
eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar vāy moḻippaṭi,
taṉittamiḻt tantip pēṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaṭku vara iruppataṟkē nēriṭṭa nērcciyām eṉka.
iyakkam
aṭikaḷ iyakkam ālamarameṉak kālpala koṇṭu akala akala viriyat taṉittamiḻ iyakkamāy, ulakattamiḻk kaḻakamāy, pāvalarēṟu tamiḻk kaḷamāy, cērar koṟṟamāy, tamiḻkkāppuk kaḻakamāy, tamiḻp pātukāppuk kaḻakamāy, taṉittamiḻ ilakkiyak kaḻakamāy, tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakamāy, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ maṉṟamāy, tamiḻkkāval kuḻuvāy, iyaṟṟamiḻp payiṟṟakamāy, pāvāṇar tamiḻ iyakkamāy āṅkāṅku mukiḻttu muṉippoṭu kaṭaṉāṟṟa ēviyatām.
nūlakam
aṭikaḷ nūṟṟoki, arumaṇik kuviyaṉṉa nālāyiram nūlkaḷik koṇṭatu. etiratu uṇarntu, civacittānta nūṟ patippuk kaḻakattiṟku muṟkoṭiyāka vaḻaṅkiyatāl, nālāyiram nāṟpatāyiramāy mēlum mēlum virintu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nūlakap peyarāl, perumimiku vaḷḷalār mutaṟkaṇ poḻivu ceyta periyavar cōmu avarkaḷ illamē vāykka ulaka vaḷamākkiyatām! ataṉ vaḻiyākavē aṭikaḷ tāmē veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷum, veḷiyiṭāmal kaḻaka vaḻi veḷivanta nūlkaḷum, aṭikaḷ kālattiṟkup piṉ veḷivanta nūlkaḷum ākiya muḻumit tokutikaḷum ki.pi. 1898 mutal 1969 vari mutaṟpatippāka veḷivantavi. avaṟṟuḷ paṭikaḷ kiṭṭutaṟku ariyaviyum uṇṭu. aṭikaḷ nūlkaḷ nāṭṭuṭimi ākkap paṭṭa pēṟṟāl, iṉṟu vāytta nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum oṭṭumottamāka vaḻaṅkum peruvāyppit tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peṟṟatām.
veḷḷac cētam
aṇmiyil ēṟpaṭṭa veḷḷap perukku patippakattuḷ pukuntu accu aṭikkappaṭṭatum, aṭikkappaṭa iruntatum, kaṇiṉippaṭutti vitta kuṟuvaṭṭukaḷum,  mūlanūlkaḷum eṉa vāri aḷḷik koṇṭu pōṉami, kaḷattukku vanta katir maṇikaḷ, kaḷañciyattiṟku vārāmal pōya, pēraḻivu oppatām. ātalāl, nūlkaḷi mīḷat tēṭavum, kaṇiṉippaṭuttavum, kuṟuvaṭṭu ākki acciṭavum eṉa mummaṭaṅkup paṇiyum pēriḻappum perutta avalamum uṇṭākkiṟṟām. anniliyil,
ūḻiyum uppakkam kāṇpar uliviṉṟit
  tāḻā tuñaṟṟu pavar
eṉṉum vāymoḻiyi vāymi moḻiyākkiya tuṇivuc celvar iḷavaḻakaṉārum avar kuṭumpattavarum āvar!
muṉṉaṟivipput tiṭṭappaṭi nūlukku ārvamāyp paṇam celuttiyōrkkum nūl kālattil kiṭṭā uḷiccal illāmal pōkātē! avarkaḷum amintu tāṅkal, iyaṟki iyaṅkiyal vaḻiyatām.
veḷiyīṭukaḷ
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam tamiḻmoḻi iṉa maṇvaḷam - vaḷarcci mīṭṭeṭuppu - eṉpavaṟṟi muṉvittut tamiḻp pōrāḷi iḷavaḻakaṉārāl toṭaṅkappaṭṭatāl, pēriḻappukkām eṉat teḷivākat terintum, icik kalikku vāytta oppaṟṟa kaḷañciyamāy vāytta - karuṇāmirta cākaratti veḷiyiṭṭatām! muṉṉi vanta mutaṟpakuti pēriḻappākkiyum ataṉ piṉṉip pakutikaḷiyum aritiṉ muyaṉṟu veḷiyiṭṭatu! moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar; īḻattu aṟiñar na.ci. kantiyā;  varalāṟṟaṟiñar cāmināta carmā, urivēntar auvi cu.tu; nāvalar na.mu.vē; paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālar; cāmi citamparaṉār; mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭa cāmiyār; pēraṟiñar ka. veḷḷi vāraṇaṉār; pāvalar kuḻantiyār; pāvalar muṭiyaracaṉār; pāvēntar, tol poruḷ kalveṭṭu āyñar catācivaṉār, cāttaṉkuḷam irākavaṉ  nūlkaḷiyum eḷiyēṉ nūlkaḷiyum muḻumi muḻumiyāka veḷiyiṭṭatu.
tolkāppiyam- pāṭṭu - toki - kāppiyam - kīḻkkaṇakku - tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu - āṅkilam valla aṟiñar irāma nātaṉār nūlkaḷiyum, yāḻppāṇa akarāti mutalām akarātit tokutikaḷiyum veḷiyiṭṭatu. patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutitoṭṭu, irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti vari veḷivanta - tamiḻukku ākkamām nāṭ ṭuṭimi ākkappaṭṭa nūlkaḷi ellām - ōriṭattup peṟṟu, oru taṉittamiḻ nūlakam amikkum aḷavukku acciṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya perumi iḷavaḻakaṉārkku uṇṭu. avarē aṉṟi avar tuṇiyiyum makkaḷiyum uṟaviyum aṉpiyum naṇpiyum oruṅkē iṇittu, aṟiñar perumakkaḷ palar aravaṇippiyum vāyppak koṇṭu vāḻum iyalpu avarkku vāyttalāl maṭṭumē, ivvāṟu ceyaṟkariya ceyya vāyttatām eṉpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tikaḻpavarum avar.
maṟimaliya māṇpu
maṟimaliyam, taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! tamiḻt taṉip peruvaḷam! muttamiḻc curappu! cīrtiruttac ceḻumi! vāḻviyal vaḷam! kalic collākkak kaḷañciyam! paṇṭit tamiḻar varalāṟṟup putiyal! āyviyal vaḻikāṭṭi! poḻivup polivu! eḻuttiṉ eḻil! moḻik kāval muṉiyam! muṭṭaṟukkum muḻutuṟu koṭi! mīṭṭeṭukkum mētakimi! aḷḷa aḷḷak kuṟiyā aṇimaṇik kuviyal! eṉal meymi viḷakkamām!
puttakam ēṟṟup polivatē puttakam
 vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
tiruvaḷḷuvar niliyam,							      iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
7, irāmaṉ teru,							                irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
tirunakar, maturi - 6

malikka vikkum maṟimaliyam!
patippuri
moḻiyē iṉamakkaḷ muṉṉēṟa nalla
vaḻiyām ataṉi vaḷarkka -paḻiyāyt
tamiḻayarat tāḻntāṉ tamiḻaṉ avaṉē
tamiḻuyarat tāṉuyarvāṉ tāṉ
moḻippēraṟiñar pāvāṇar avarkaḷ `moḻiyiṉ vaḻiyākavē moḻipēcum makkaḷ muṉṉēṟa muṭiyum eṉak kūṟukiṟār. ulakam muḻutum ivvuṇmi uṇarappaṭṭuḷḷati varalāṟu kāṭṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇari aṟivulakam cariyāka aṭiyāḷam kāṇpataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉatu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār tanta pārāṭṭu!
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ (15.7.1876 - 15.9.1950) tēvanēyap pāvāṇar (7.2.1902 - 16.1.1981) iruvarum taṉittamiḻiyakkattit tāṅki niṟkum tūṇkaḷākap pōṟṟappaṭupavarkaḷ.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār 1916il tōṟṟuvitta taṉittamiḻ iyakkam, nūṟṟāṇṭi eṭṭiyuḷḷa makiḻvāṉa nēram itu!
taṉittamiḻiyakka nūṟṟāṇṭi niṉivu kūrum vakiyil, taṉittamiḻiyakkat tanti maṟimaliyaṭikaḷāriṉ eḻuttu, coṟpoḻivu, nāṭkuṟippu, kaṭitaṅkaḷ aṉittiyum `maṟimaliyam eṉum peruntokuppākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam vaḻi veḷikkoṇarat tiṭṭamiṭṭōm.
ulakam muḻutum paravi vāḻum uṇarvu kuṉṟāta tamiḻarkaḷ tanta ūkkuvippu, intap peruntiṭṭatti niṟivēṟṟit tīravēṇṭum eṉum uṟutiyi eṉakkut tantatu.
taṉittamiḻ iyakka aṟiñarkaḷum ārvalarkaḷum aṭikaḷāriṉ ariya paṭippukaḷ palavaṟṟiyum tēṭittēṭi vaḻaṅki vaḻikāṭṭiṉar; valimiyūṭṭiṉar.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār 1898ām āṇṭiliruntu eḻuti vantuḷḷār. aṟivukkaṭal (ñāṉacākaram) itaḻi 1902ām āṇṭiliruntu veḷiyiṭṭu vantuḷḷār. avariṉ paṭippukaḷ peruveḷḷamāy veḷivantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
accēṟātavi, peyar kūṭak kēṭṭaṟiyātavi, peyar terintum pārvikkuk kiṭṭātavi - eṉap palavakiyāyk kiṭittaṉa aṭikaḷāriṉ arumpaṭippukaḷ! avaṟṟi `maṟimaliyam eṉum talippil 34 tokuppukaḷāka orucērap pārkkumpōtu, tamiḻulakam viyappaṭivatu uṟuti.
moḻinalam iṉanalam pēṇutal aṉṟō
muḻunalam peṟuvaḻi! nāmpeṟal eṉṟō?
ippaṭik kavaliyōṭu kēṭpār pāvalarēṟu peruñ cittiraṉār (10.3.1933 - 11.6.1995). avariṉ `teṉmoḻi itaḻ 1.8.1959 iliruntu veḷivarat toṭaṅkiya piṉputāṉ, taṉittamiḻt tici nōkki nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉōr naṭipōṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. atil nāṉum oruvaṉ.
peruñcittiraṉāriṉ eḻuttu kāṭṭiya aṭiyāḷattāl, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ eṉum aṟivupputiyal palar kikaḷilum kuṭiyēṟiyatu.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ valiyuṟuttip pēciyatāl, maṟimali yaṭikaḷārit tēṭippayilum ārvam tamiḻulakil puttuyir peṟṟatu.
āriyatti etirppatilum camaṟkirutatti vilakku vatilumē tamiḻiṉa mēmpāṭum tamiḻk kāppum aṭaṅkiyirukkiṟatu. avvuṇarcciyit tamiḻar neñcaṅkaḷil arumpa vittatu tirāviṭar iyakkam; malarntu viriyavittatu taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! kumuka, araciyal, paṇpāṭṭu malarccikku inta īriyakkaṅkaḷiṉ pēruḻippup payaṉpaṭṭatu.
tamiḻakattil taṉittamiḻ uṇarvu ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ taḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu; 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ cariyat toṭaṅkiyatu. vaṭamoḻi veḷḷattil tamiḻ iḻuttuc cellappaṭṭu viṭāmal kāppāṟṟiyōr kamparum pavaṇantiyum! piṉvantōrukku antak kāppuṇarcci illāmal, ayaṉ moḻip paṟṟukku aṭimiyāṉatāl tamiḻum tamiḻiṉamum peṟṟa iḻappukaḷ ērāḷam!
ayaṉmoḻic cēṟṟiliruntu tamiḻi mīṭkum muyaṟci āṅkilēyar varukiyiyoṭṭip puttuyir peṟṟatu. vaḷḷalār, pāmpaṉ cāmikaḷ, vaṇṇac carapam taṇṭapāṇi cuvāmikaḷ, cuppiramaṇiyacivā, paritimāṟ kaliñar mutaliya palarum toṭakka vitikaḷāyiṉar.
kōvi avināciyil piṟantu virutunakaril vāḻnta viruti civañāṉayōki 19.11.1908 ām nāḷ `tiruviṭar kaḻakam toṭaṅkit taṉittamiḻ uṇarccikku vēkaṅkūṭṭiṉār. avvamippil uṟuppiṉarāyiruntavar cuvāmi vētācalam (malimaliyaṭikaḷ).
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉat tam peyarik koṇṭu, 1916il avar toṭaṅkiyatē taṉittamiḻ iyakkam.
`tuṟimiku āṅkilam maṟivaṭa moḻiyoṭu
 tūytamiḻp pēraṟiñar - taṉittamiḻ
 tōṟṟiya ōraṟiñar
pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār pāṭiyataṟkēṟpa, maṟimali yaṭikaḷār tamiḻ, āṅkilam, camaṟkirutam mutaliya paṉmoḻiyilum pulami mikkavar.
paṉmoḻip pēraṟiñarāṉa maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum ātaravāka niṉṟatāl etirppukkum iḻappukkum āḷākkappaṭṭār. āṉālum uṟuti kuṉṟāmal tamiḻukku ātaravāy uḻikkum uḷḷattōṭiruntār. ataṉāl, tamiḻar uḷḷaṅkaḷil iṉṟaḷavum māṟāp perumiyōṭu avar vīṟṟirukkiṉṟār.
vāḻttāta nāḷilli viyakam
  maṟimali yaṭikaḷ maṟiyāt tiruppeyar
  vāḻttāta nāḷilli viyakam
pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avar māṇpiṉip pāṭi makiḻntatum, tam `kuṭumpa viḷakku nūliṉi maṟimali yaṭikaḷukkuk kāṇikkiyākki vaḻaṅkiyatum, avariṉ uṟuti taḷarāta uḷḷamum uḻippum kaṇṭu pōṟṟiya naṉṟik kaṭaṉ eṉpatil ayyamilli.
ceṉṉiyil kaṭanta irupatu āṇṭukaḷākac ceyalpaṭṭu varum tamiḻmaṇ patippakap paṇikaḷit tamiḻulakam aṟiyum.
vāḻum talimuṟikkum varuṅkālat talimuṟikkum vaḻikāṭṭavalla tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ palariṉ nūlkaḷi muḻumuḻut tokuppukaḷāka vaḻaṅkiyiruppatu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ muṭippiṉi (cātaṉi)yākap pōṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku vittiṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷik kaṇṭaṟintu, avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷi muḻumiyāṉa tokuppukaḷākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟatu. avi kālam muḻutum payaṉpaṭavalla karuvi nūlkaḷ eṉpati aṟiñar ulakam aṟiyum!
tamatu nalatti otukki vittu, tamiḻ nalattik kākka vāḻnta aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṉittu nūlkaḷiyum oru cērat tokuttu muḻut tokuppāka vaḻaṅkuvatu perumpaṇi! ataṉit tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭarntu niṟivēṟṟi varukiṟatu. tokuppu nūlkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalip piṉṉiṇippil kāṇka.
tamiḻukkāka uḻikkum uṇarvi iḷamiyil emmiṭam ēṟpaṭuttiyōruḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉārum avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉārum kuṟippiṭattakkōr! em naṉṟikkuriyōr!
taṉittamiḻ ārvam, tirāviṭar iyakka īṭupāṭu eṉum iru ciṟakukaḷāl emmi eḻavittavar perumpulavar nakkīraṉār! avari eṇṇik kaṇpaṉikka naṉṟi kūṟukiṟatu em neñcam.
kālappaḻamiyālum parāmarippuk kuṟivālum aḻiya nērnta tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ aḷavaṟṟavi! avaṟṟil orupakutiyi yāvatu mīṭṭu nam kikaḷukku vaḻaṅkiyōr āṟumukanāvalar, ci.vi. tāmōtaraṉār, u.vē.cāminātar mutaliya cāṉṟōr perumakkaḷ!
tamiḻilakkiyap peruñcelvatti mīṇṭum mīṇṭum accākki, aṉivar kikaḷiyum eṭṭac ceyta perumi - civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, u.vē.cā. nūlakam, maṇivācakar patippakam mutaliya patippāḷarkaḷukku uriyatu.
accuttuṟi aṟimukamāṉa piṉpē, aṟivuttuṟi aṉivarukkum potuccottākum vāyppu vantatu. eṭṭāk kaṉiyāka ōlic cuvaṭikaḷil muṭaṅkik kiṭanta ilakkiyac celvaṅkaḷ, ellōrukkum eḷitāyk kiṭikkum paṇiyic ceyta patippāciriyarkaḷum, patippāḷarkaḷum nam naṉṟik kuriyōr!
iravu pakal pārātu mutumiyik karutātu uḻikkum maṉavuratti muṉṉōṭip patippāciriyarkaḷiṉ paṇiyē emakku vaḻaṅkiyatu.
aracum, palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷum, aṟaniṟuvaṉaṅkaḷum, camaya pīṭaṅkaḷum ,perum iyakkaṅkaḷum, perum celvarkaḷum ceyya vēṇṭiya perun tamiḻppaṇi itu! eḷiyavarkaḷāl iyaṅkum tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peruntamiḻp paṇikaḷi iṉṉalkaḷukku iṭiyil arumuyaṟciyōṭu ceytu varuvatu, uḷḷavuram oṉṟiṉāltāṉ!
vallami cērkkum valimiyuṇ ṭākkum!
  vaṇṭamiḻ nintiṭil etunammik kākkum?
kākkum tamiḻik kākkum uṇarvip pāvēntar pāṭal varikaḷ tantaṉa.
accukku eṭṭāmalum kikkuk kiṭṭāmalum irunta pēraṟiñar palariṉ nūlkaḷi nāṭṭuṭimiyākki tirāviṭar iyakka aracukaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ataṉāl, aṟivu paravalākum vāyppukaḷ uruvāyiṉa.
`carivum iḻappum namakku vantālum tamiḻukku varakkūṭātu eṉa eṇṇi uḻikkum iyakkaṅkaḷum amippukaḷum tamiḻ nāṭṭil vaṟṟāmal vaḷarntapaṭi irukkiṉṟaṉa.

taṉittamiḻ iyakkam (1916)
teṉṉintiya nalurimik kaḻakam (1916)

taṉittamiḻk kaḻakam (1964)
ulakattamiḻk kaḻakam (1968)
tamiḻiyakkam (1972)
tamiḻc cāṉṟōr pēravi (1998)
tamiḻ malarccikkum taṉittamiḻ vaḷarccikkum aṭittaḷam amitta ittaku amippukaḷukku mūla viciyākat tikaḻum perumiyip peṟuvōr maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārum pāvāṇarumē!
tamiḻ vaḷattiṟkum tamiḻar nalattiṟkum civaṉiya mēmpāṭṭiṟkum maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār āṟṟiya paṇikaḷ aḷappariyavi. avi toṭarpāka avar āyntaḷitta aṟivu muttukaḷ eṇṇaṟṟavi!
aṭikaḷāriṉ aṉittup paṭippukaḷum poruḷvaḻip pirikkappaṭṭu, kālavariciyil 34 tokuppukaḷākat tokukkap paṭṭu `maṟimaliyam eṉa uṅkaḷ kikaḷil ippōtu tavaḻkiṉṟatu.
pala talimuṟikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum aṟivuk karuvūlam itu!
tamiḻar illantōṟum irukkavēṇṭiya aruntamiḻc celvam itu!
aṟivulakattāl `maṟimaliyam varavēṟkappaṭum eṉṉum nampikki emakkuṇṭu. nūṟ curaṅkattil nuḻiya aḻikkiṟōm.

  tamiḻkkeṉa vāḻvatē vāḻvatākum
eṉum pāvēntar varikaḷi niṉivuk kūṟukiṟōm.

patippāḷar vivaram
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ 
piṟanta nāḷ	:	3.7.1948
piṟanta ūr	:	uṟantirāyaṉkuṭikkāṭu añcal						urattanāṭu vaṭṭam - 614 625							tañcāvūr māvaṭṭam.
kalvi	:	kallūri pukumuka vakuppu
ippōtiya toḻil	:	puḻakkattil illāta paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷit 					tēṭiyeṭuttu veḷiyiṭal
āṟṟiyuḷḷa potuppaṇikaḷ
1965il paḷḷi māṇavaṉāka iruntapōtē moḻip pōrāṭṭattil muṉippākap paṅkēṟṟut taḷippaṭuttap peṟṟu 48 nāḷkaḷ ciṟiyil iruntavar.
piṟanta ūrākiya uṟantirāyaṉkuṭikkāṭṭil `ūrnalaṉ vaḷarccik kaḻakam eṉum camūka amippil iruntu ūr nalappaṇi āṟṟiyavar.
urattanāṭṭil `tamiḻar urimik kaḻakam eṉṉum amippiyum, pāvāṇar paṭippakattiyum naṇparkaḷuṭaṉ iṇintu niṟuvit tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻiṉa, tamiḻaka mēmpāṭṭiṟku uḻittavar. iḷam talimuṟikkut tamiḻt toṇṭāṟṟiyavar.
pēraṟiñar aṇṇāviṉ matuvilakkuk koḷkiyi neñcil ēnti urattanāṭu matuvilakkuk kuḻuviṉ mukkiya amippāḷarkaḷil oruvarāka iruntu ceyalāṟṟiyavar. 1975-il tamiḻnāṭu caṭṭamaṉṟattil `urattanāṭu tiṭṭam eṉṟu pārāṭṭap peṟṟa matuvilakkut tiṭṭam veṟṟi peṟa uḻittavar.
tamiḻmaṇ patippakatti niṟuvi puḻakkattil illāta  paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyum, putiya paṭippu ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum, 19  ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkattilum vāḻnta tamiḻccāṉṟōrkaḷ eḻuti vittuc ceṉṟa tamiḻiṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷi tēṭi eṭuttu muḻumiyākac cērttu poruḷvaḻip pirittu, kāla niralil orē vīccil toṭar toṭarāka  veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippil taṉi muttiri patittu varupavar.
potunili
taṉittamiḻ iyakkat tanti maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ, tanti periyār, pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ, pēraṟiñar aṇṇā, moḻinūl mūtaṟiñar ñā. tēvanēyap pāvāṇar,  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār ākiyōri vaḻikāṭṭikaḷākak koṇṭu avartam koḷkikaḷi niṟivēṟṟa ayarātu uḻittu varupavar.

maṟimaliyam tokuppil iṭampeṟāta nūlkaḷ
mutaṟkuṟaḷ vāta nirākaraṇam (1898), tukaḷaṟu pōtam (1898), cittānta ñāṉapōtam catamaṇik kōvi kuṟippuri (1898), vēta civākamap piramāṇyam (1900),  inta nāṉku nūlkaḷum tamiḻakattiṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷil tēṭiyum eṅkaḷ kikkuk kiṭikka villi. ātalāṉ, maṟimaliyam tokuppil mēṟkaṇṭa nūlkaḷ iṭam peṟavilli.

nūluri
iḷiñarkkāṉa iṉṟamiḻ

itaṉ mutaṟpatippu aṭikaḷār viruppa āvaṇappaṭi 1957 il veḷivantatu.
paḷḷik kūṭaṅkaḷil payilum ciṟārkku iṉiya eḷiya tiruttamāṉa taṉittamiḻil eḻutappaṭṭa pāṭanūlkaḷ illāp peruṅkuṟiyi nīkkum poruṭṭu 1934 il ciṟuvarkkāṉa centamiḻ eṉṉum nūl eḻutiyatic cuṭṭum aṭikaḷ nōkkik kuṟippiṭṭu, itukāl iḷiñarkkāṉa iṉṟamiḻ veḷiyiṭuvatip patippuri veḷippaṭuttukiṟatu.
tiṅkaḷit toḻutal mutalāka makkaḷ vāḻkki īṟāka 13 kaṭṭurikaḷiyuṭiyatu iḷiñarkkāṉa iṉṟamiḻ.
iṉimi tantu makiḻvippatāl, `tiṅkaḷ eṉṟum,
nāḷum vaḷartalāl, `kaliyōṉ eṉṟum,
iravil viḷaṅkutalāl `iravōṉ eṉṟum, alavaṉ eṉṟum
`allōṉ eṉṟum
kālavari yaṟi ceytalāl `mati eṉṟum
iṭi iṭiyē kaṟiyuṭimiyāl, `kaḷaṅkaṉ eṉṟum piṟi niliyil vaḷinta tōṟṟam tarutalāl, `kuraṅki eṉṟum ak kaṟi muyalpōl tōṟṟam tarutalāl, `muyaṟkūṭu eṉṟum peyar peṟum eṉpatip poruḷoṭu puṇarttu viḷakkukiṟār.
tēytal uṇmiyum perukal uṇmiyum
  māytal uṇmiyum piṟattal uṇmiyum
  aṟiyā tōriyum aṟiyak kāṭṭum
arumiyi uṟiyūr mutukaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉār pāṭiya puṟappāṭal vaḻiyil kāṭṭukiṟār.
ivvāṟē eṭutta poruḷiṉ peyar viḷakkam - payaṉ viḷakkam - cāṉṟu viḷakkam eṉak kāṭṭik kaṭṭuri eḻutum payiṟciyil vallarām  vakiyil nūl yāttuḷār.
`niliyāṉam eṉpati, `nerunal uḷaṉoruvaṉ iṉṟilli eṉpataṟku maṭṭum 14 cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭukiṟār.
`kālatiyaṉ kiricelāṉ kati eṉpatoru kati iṭam peṟukiṟatu. ittāliya nāṭṭuk kati. ik kati virivuṭiyatu. maṇaṅ koḷḷa virumpāta maṉṉaṉi nāṭṭumakkaḷ maṇaṅkoḷḷa vaṟpuṟutta, avaṉ. yāṉ eṉṉuṭiya kaṇkaḷik koṇṭē oru maṇamakaḷit tēṭip peṟa vēṇṭumē aṉṟip piṟaruṭiya kaṇkaḷik koṇṭu avaḷit tēṭi aṭital kūṭātu eṉṟu kūṟi avvāṟē tēṭi aṭintu, mēṉmiyuṟṟa kati atuvām. paḻantamiḻar kātal vāḻviṉ `piḻicāṟu aṉiyatu itu.
tiruvaḷḷuva māli - kapilar akaval mutalām piṟkālap puṉivil aṭikaḷ nampikki koṇṭatu, nampamuṭiyātatākavē uḷḷami uṇmiyām! tiruvaḷḷuvar, tiruvaḷḷuvar vāḻkki, aṟañceyki, vaḷḷuvar, vaḷḷuvam paṟṟiyavi.
kāṉat tōki tēvamaṇi kati eṉpatu, uḷḷotta kātalarākat tikaḻnta kāṉat tōkiyum tēvamaṇiyum, avarkaḷ peṟṟērkaḷāl veṟṟipeṟa iyalā niliyil, iruvarum orutuṟavumaṭam cārntu oruvari oruvar aṟiyārāy vāḻntu, aṟinta piṉṉarum attuṟavil nilitta peruvāḻvu urippatām. nallapaṭippu. maṉōṉmaṇīyam cuntaraṉār paṭippāṉa civakāmi caritattiṉ cāyal ik katiyamippil pulappaṭukiṉṟatu!

accaṭippu maṟṟum nūl kaṭṭamippu:  
veṅkaṭēcuvarā āpceṭ piriṇṭarcu maṟṟum pirācacu intiyā						
`maṟimaliyam ellā niliyilum ceppamuṟa veḷi varuvataṟkup pallāṟṟāṉum utaviyāka irunta ivarkaḷukkum maṟṟum aṟiñar perumakkaḷukkum em niṟinta  naṉṟiyum pārāṭṭum.

ciṟuvarkkāṉa centamiḻ
1964il pāri niliyam mūṉṟām patippāka veḷiyiṭṭa 
nūliṉi mūlamākak koṇṭu innūl patippikkappaṭukiṟatu.
innūlip paṟṟiya kuṟippuri..
eḷiya naṭiyilum, paḷḷic ciṟār payaṉmikap peṟum vakiyilum, tamiḻaṟivu taḻikkum vakiyilum innūl amintuḷḷatu. ciṟuvarkkāṉa kaṭamikaḷik kūṟum aṭikaḷār, ciṟu katikaḷ palavaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyākac cintaṉikaḷi vaḷart tuḷḷār. aṭikaḷār colvaḷaṉum kaṟpaṉi vaḷaṉum innūṟ ceytikaḷukkut tuṇi niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
āyvu neṟikaḷiyum āyvup payaṉkaḷiyum āyvāḷar iyalpukaḷiyum aṭikaḷār viḷakki yuḷḷār. ulaka nikaḻccikaḷi uṟṟunōkki ārāyum āyvē, uṇmip payaṉmiku āyvākum.
centamiḻvaḻi ciṟuvarkkāṉa ciṟanta karuttu kaḷi  aṭikaḷār aṉpu vaṭivilum kūṟiyuḷḷār. nalleṇṇamum naṉ muyaṟciyum koṇṭāl celvattiyum civattiṉ aruḷiyum koḷ ḷalām eṉak kūṟum innūlukku ārvantūṇṭum aṟivārnta āṅkila muṉṉuri mēlum aḻaku cērkkiṟatu.
- nā. ceyappirakācu
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷāriṉ 
ilakkiyap paṭippukaḷ


Preface
“Tamil can readily dispense with the greater part or whole of Sanscrit, and by dispensing with it rises to a purer and more refined style, where as English cannot abandon its Latin without abandoning perspicuity.:” -Dr. Caldwell in his “Comparative Grammar of the Dravidian Languages,” 3rd edition, P.47.
This great truth discriminatively perceived and impartially proclimed by Dr. Caldwell the greatest Dravidian philologist, concerning the richness of the Tamil language and the poverty of the other, it is regrettable even Tamil scholars of the land have not sufficiently recognised. How rich, pure and elegant in words and phrases the ancient Tamil literature had been, how simple, yet how lovely, original, edifying and true to nature had the conceptions of its poets and prose writers shone, but to what a poor, mixed, inelegant and low style has the language of its modern literatue fallen, and to what an artificial, ugly, slavish, corrupted and false mode of strin the thoughts and ideas of its authors are fettered, a slight comparison of the two literatures will easily disclose. As has been truly observed by J.A. Symonds, “The genius of a language is the genius of the race which made it.” This significant fact as applying to Tamil cannot be expressed so well as in the notable works of Dr. Gilbert Slater who writes thus: “The very character of the Tamil language, the perfection with which it has been developed into an organ for precise and subtle thought, combined with the fact that it represents a much earlier stage in the evolution of inflexional language than any Indo-Germanic tongue, suggests, though of course it does not prove, the priority of the Dravidians in attining settled order and regular government.” (The Dravidian Element in Indian Culture, p 70) Though Dr. Slater, in climing “a greater antiquity for Dravidian than for Aryan civilisation”, only suggests, from a correct understanding of the perfect structure of Tamil, the fact that the Tamilians had attined to a settled order of society and government long before the Aryan people settled into order yet an attentive study of the very ancient Tamil work the Tholkappiam and such old Tamil classics as the Pathuppattu, the Purananuru and others will afford proofs indisputable for substantiating what the learned doctor has hinted above.
As the primary function of language is to facilitate the communication of ideas among a people by means of sounds commonly recognised by them, as the growth of civilisation among them can increase only in proportion as they imbibe the thoughts and ideas of their own inteligent men and make them the guiding principle of life, and as this intermingling of thoughts and ideas cannot take place but through their own recognised, connected, significant sounds called their mother tongue, it had become absolutely necessary for them to preserve their language from constant change and decay and make its usage pure and stable. It is only in the midst of peoples who thus advance in civilisation that the necessity of keeping their tongue pure, undefiled and fixed is deeply felt. Others who take no effort at all to better their condition but are content with the mere satisfaction of their animal appetites, care so little to stamp the usage of their speech that in a round of ten or twenty years a new languge comes to life among them and in another round it dies away. After quoting many instances to show how languages, like mushrooms, rise and perish rapidly among savages, the great and distinguished linguist Prof. Max Muller says: “We read of missionaries in Central America who attempted to write down the language of savage tribes, and who compiled with great care a dictionary of all the words they could lay hold of. Returning to the same tribe after the lapse of only ten years, they found that this dictionary had become antiquated and useless. Old words had sunk to the ground and new ones had arisen to the surface; an to all outward appearance the language was completely changed.”  (The science of Language, 1899, Vol. I. p66) It must be obvious then how important it is for a civilized people to keep their language intact and mintin its purity to an utmost extent. Still, however civilized a people may be, all the persons who compose it cannot exert themselves to keep their language pure and fixed since laziness in an inborn principle in many which renders them indifferent to a correct pronunciation of words and sentences. And is close association with this principle of laziness which inwardly undermines the structure and stability of a language, another equally baneful, springing from the desire for imitation, comes rather outwardly from the contact of two peoples speaking or using two different languages, and induces them to import the words and phrases of the one into the other. Unless the mischief played by these two evil principles is checked strongly and in time, a language cannot live long but will quickly pass away and with it all scope for culture and civilisation will also pass away.
Now where can we look for a potent power to come from and check the operation of the two evils but from the small but active and intelligent section of a people which forms the centre radiating knowledge and civilisation al around. If a people could be so fortunate, like the Tamilians or the Egyptians, as to possess early among them an intelligent class who, by creating a literature and committing it to writing, made a persistent effort to check the decay of their language and the promiscuous mixture of foreign elements in it, their language, theirs alone, would acquire a vitality that would keep it permanently living, But for the creation and existence of a large and rich literature possessing a many-sided interest for its people, it would not have been possible to arrest the change and decay of a language. Prof. Sayce pertinently well observes: “Destroy literature and facility of intercommunication, and the languages of England and America would soon be as different as those of France and Italy” (The science of Languageï 1900, Vol p 210).
When one grasps the importance of this circumstance which controls the fate and destiny of a language it would become clear to his or her mind why Tamil has lived so long and still continues to live in all its youthful vigour, even after such cultured languages of high antiquity as the Egyptian, the Accadian, the Assyrian, the Hebrew, the Aryan and so on have ceased to exist. So far    as we can compute on historical grounds, it is for more than ten thousand years that the Tamil language is being spoken and written. And to invigorate its constitution in addition to its innate vitality it possesses a vast, varied, original, useful and highly interesting literatue produced from 5000 B.C. up to the present times. The great and momentous fact must be borne in mind that, of the few cultivated languages of the ancient world, Tamil alone, as I just pointed out, is still living in all its richness and youthful vigour, all others of the modern world being only five or six hundred years old and even among these, except some European languages, few own original literatures as pure Tamil.
To many it might look a surprising circumstances that Tamil should outlive all other ancient languages, that it should still display so much virility as argues for its perennial existence. But the secret is not far to seek, although it has eluded the notice of even erudite scholars. The words, phrases and sentences of Tamil require but little effort on the part of the speaker to utter them properly. The sounds of each letter and word issue forth from the throat and mouth normally and naturally, giving no trouble whatever to the speaker. That all its twelve vowels and eighteen consonants constitute the only natural and normal sounds that could come out from the human voice with the least effort, can be shown clearly by inquiring into the phonetic and physiological laws that lie at their basis; but in a short preface like this it is not possible to enter into that profitable study. We have already shown that laziness in pronunciation forms one of the min factors that lead to the constant change, decay and death of languages. But in the case of Tamil, however lazy a man might be, he cannot pronounce its words so badly as to efface their identity altogether. On the other hand, the words themselves, without requiring much effort, flow out from him like the rhythm of a sweet tune issuing forth from the strings of a harp touched by summer breeze. For the Tamil lanugage does not possess such hard consonants, hissing sounds and aspirates as are possessed by Sanscrit, Hebrew and other cognate languages. It is this distinct  melliflous character of its sounds that has preserved and still preserves Tamil from any disastrous change and decay. To this day even the illiterate Tamil peasants understand the classical poetic language of the Thirukkural that was composed at the beginning of the Christian era. When I had been at Jaffna during my lecturing tour, I was struck with wonder when I heard from the lips of its people many pure Tamil words used in ancient Tamil classics, but which have fallen into disuse in Madras and suburban cities. Even big cities like Madras where all sorts of people speaking all sorts of languages mix and mingle together, all those who speak Tamil speak it purely whether they be the born Tamils or others who have adopted it merely for social intercourse or business purposes. In the colloquial Tamil scarcely one percent of either Sanscrit or other foreign words mixes.
But the mischief that affects it badly comes minly from the circle of English-knowing brahmin and non-brahmin officials, who speak neither pure Tamil nor pure English, but mix the two together so badly and ridiculously that neither a Tamilian nor an Englishman understands their language but turns aside with disgust. Besides introducing into their spoken and written Tamil many an English word and phrase, nay even whole sentences, the brahmins and their followers import into it Sanscrit and Hindustani terms also. This “constant degradation of language” in the words of Pater, “by those who use it carelessly,” at present affects Tamil so wholly by the slipshod fashion in which it is handled by the so called educated people. Whether your speak English, or whether you speak Tamil or any other language, you must learn to use it in such a way as to make your ideas quite intelligible to the people who are born and bred in that language. The standard by which one ought to measure the nature and use of a language must be the extent of its usage among the largest portion of its people in the dily intercourse of their life. As the  educated section forms a very small minority of almost all the people in the world, especially in this country not only is it an inconsiderable part, but it also concerns itself less about the welfare of the masses than its own, the present fashionable mode of their easy talk cannot, with any propriety, be taken as the standard for using a language like Tamil which has been cultivated for more than fifty centuries and is spoken still by more than twentyone millions in Southern India and Ceylon alone. As days advance with the spread  of English education, the gulf between the educated and the uneducated Tamils also widens, their being  little understanding between them and little sympathy with each other. The root-cuse of this may, by all careful observers, be traced to the inadequate instruction imparted in Tamil to students reading from the High School to College classes.
The object of the following treatise therefore is to remedy this defect at first by feeding properly the acquisition of the knowledge of Tamil at its very root, and secondly by acquainting the young mind not only with a knowledge of actual facts but also with practical moral maxims clothed, of course, in the attractive allegorical form called the fable.
To attin my first object the whole work is written in pure but simple Tamil, only here and there so interspersed with the words and phrases taken from the classical Tamil literature, as to make their meaning easily understood by their context and help the students in this way early to store in their memory a considerable number of fine and beautiful words and expressions they will frequently meet with in their later course of higher Tamil studies. Although I have in this prose work scrupulously avoided quoting any poetic, yet the synonyms given at the opening of each original essay and the classical terms and expression used here and there, will, I hope, greatly contribute to a sound knowledge of Tamil, of its structure and peculiarities, and aid the student to understand any poetical composition also to some extent. The manner of writing employed in this book, besides increasing the student’s knowledge  of Tamil, will also enrich his Tamil vocabulary and serve him in good stead to communicate easily to his countrymen the ideas he has imbibed from the various branches of English learning.
My second object is to strike out a new path in the treatment of subjects, keeping in view the wide and amazing extension of knowledge achieved by the European nation, whole still clinging to some extent to the ancient mode of classifying subjects as lid down in the great Tamil grammar and rhetoric the Tholkappiam. Almost all the Tamil texts now in use never deviate from the beaten track of repeating the mythical stories of the Ramayana and the Mahabharata, as if the incidents related in them had been actual historical occurrences and of omitting for the most part real daring adventures and soul-inspiring deeds of the heroes who actually existed in countries other than India. By this blind belief and persistent treatment of worn - out myths , by this wanton neglect doggedly mintined towards the actual greatness of the heroes of other countries, these Tamil texts, at the very time of its genial growth, blunt the historic sense of the pupils and breed in its stead an undesirable credulity which never leaves them even after years of their excellent culture in English. I have, therefore, completely left out all kinds of Indian myths in this books and brought in minly actual historical incidents and a few supernatural events related in the reliable works in English.
In rooting up blind belief and credulity from the mind of the young pupils, it is not my desire that they should be tught to disbelieve completely all occurrences of a wonderful and supernatural character. On the other hand, it is my earnest wish that they should be instructed so as to acquaint themselves early not only with what occur ordinarily within nature and human nature but also with those that occur rarely at fitful intervals within, near and beyond them, provided they belong to the class of cases proved by careful, intelligent and disinterested witnesses. Here too the students must be on their guard, lest they should fall into the error of extending theit belief  from one proved supernatural and superhuman case to many an unproved one. This careful cultivation of mind, while it is plastic, will tend to make it susceptible of receiving into it only those ideas that are true, real and wholesome, and free from deceptions and will conduct it ultimately to and region where truth alone shine. For, greater than truth there is nothing to be attined by the human soul.
Finally, it is my sacred duty to emphasize the importance of diligently cultivating a pure Tamil style both in speaking and writing, for that alone could urge the student to stretch his vision over the vast domin of the very valuable old Tamil literature in search of new words and expressions which lie abundantly there and which will readily serve him to express his precious new ideas in Tamil without seeking the aid of any foreign tongue. The more one exercises his mind in expressing himself clearly in his own mother tongue, the more will his powers of concentration and expression increase, and the better can he serve his country, language and religion. Further, that each and every word of pure Tamil bears on its face the mark of elegant use to which it was put for more than six thousand years by the very intelligent and highly civilized forefathers of the Tamil people, that these words, instead of becoming worn out and unfit for further use like the words of the dead languages, have, on the contrary, gined immense vitality and fresh life as times went on, every patriotic son of Tamil must impress on his mind indelibly. No filial son of Tamil can discard this priceless heritage of his own and go seeking after the aid of others that belong to those who bear no good will towards him, without degrading himself into beggarry and bringing contempt on his great forefathers and on the great language they used and cherished with so much love. Dear sons of Tamil, awake from this negligent torpor, serve your mother with filial piety and sincere heart, and reinstate her once pure and undimmed glory!
Pallavaram,			          Vedachalam
25th March, 1934

1.  pakalavaṉ vaṇakkam
pakalavaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻc col cūriyaṉik kuṟippatākum. cūriyaṉ eṉṉum col vaṭanāṭṭil irunta āriyar vaḻaṅkiya vaṭacol ākum. itteṉṉāṭṭil uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷ paḻiya kālattiliruntu pakalavaṉi ñāyiṟu, katiravaṉ, cuṭaravaṉ, pariti, eṉṟūḻ, kaṉali, ellōṉ, veyilōṉ, veyyōṉ mutalāṉa pala centamiḻp peyarkaḷāl vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟaṉar.
irākkālattil tūkkattil irunta nām, pakalavaṉ kiḻakkē tōṉṟum viṭiyaṟkāliyil kaṇviḻittu eḻuntu, avaṉip pārkkum pōtu evvaḷavu makiḻcci aṭintavarkaḷāy niṟkiṉṟōm. kiḻakkē kaṭal maṭṭattiṟku mēl ñāyiṟu vaṭṭamāyp paḷapaḷa eṉṟu varukiyil aḵtu oru poṉ uruṇṭiyip pōl tōṉṟukiṉṟa taṉṟō? appōtu ataṉic cūḻa atiliruntu pāyum oḷi pallāyirak
kaṇakkāṉa poṟkampikaḷ pōl nīṇṭu cellutalip pāruṅkaḷ!
irāppoḻutil eṅkum paraviyirunta iruḷit turattik koṇṭu katiravaṉ vāṉattiṉmēl eḻumpum intak kāṭciyi nām kāṇkiyil, atu civanta tirumēṉi uṭiyaṉākiya murukappirāṉ, kaṟutta uruvattiṉi uṭiya arakkar kūṭṭattiṉ mēl pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa taṉ ampukaḷi ēvi avarkaḷit turattik koṇṭu varuvatu
pōl tōṉṟukiṉṟataṉṟō? mikunta oḷiyuṭaṉ viḷaṅkum accuṭaravaṉukkuk kīḻē pacci niṟattuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭum kaṭalāṉatu ammurukappirāṉ ēṟiyirukkum paccimayil pōlavum, akkaṭalil eḻuntu eḻuntu kīḻē viḻuntu ulāvum alikaḷāṉavi anta mayil iṟakkikaḷi virittu aṭittup paṟantu varuvatu pōlavum tōṉṟutali nōkkuṅkaḷ!
iṅṅaṉamāka, appariti vāṉavaṉatu tōṟṟam, oḷivaṭivāṉa kaṭavuḷiṉ tōṟṟattiyē ottirukkiṉṟatu. atu allāmalum, akkaṭavuḷ ellā uyirkaḷiyum aṟiyāmi eṉṉum iruḷiṉiṉṟum viṭuvittu, avaṟṟiṉ aṟivi viḷaṅkac ceytal pōla, iv eṉṟūḻum uṟakkattilirunta ellā uyirkaḷiṉ aṟiviyum eḻuppi, avikaḷip palavaki muyaṟcikaḷil ēvukiṉṟatu pāruṅkaḷ! kaṉali tōṉṟukiṟa viṭiyaṟkālattil kōḻi kūvukiṉṟatu, kākki karikiṉṟatu, māntōppukaḷiṉ ucci
yilē kuyiliṉ iṉiya oli kēṭkiṟaṉtu. iṉṉum ettaṉiyō paṟavik kūṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ ettaṉiyō vakiyāṉa ōcikaḷum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. iravellām koṭṭilil kiṭanta ākkaḷ (pacukkaḷ)  tokuti tokutiyāka veḷi nilaṅkaḷil pulmēyap pōkiṉṟaṉa. uḻavarkaḷ erutukaḷi ēriṟpūṭṭi vayalkaḷi uḻat tuvaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.
iravu muḻuvatum tūkkamayakkattuṭaṉ carakkuvaṇṭikaḷ ōṭṭi vanta vaṇṭikkārarkaḷ accarakkup potikaḷip paṇṭacāli muṟṟaṅkaḷil iṟakkukiṉṟaṉar. palvakip poruḷkaḷi viṟkum kaṭikkārarkaḷ ellām tattam kaṭikaḷi varici variciyākat tiṟantu vittu, avikaḷi vāṅka varuvārkku viṟpaṉi ceykiṉṟaṉar. palvakik kittoḻilāḷarkaḷum tattam toḻiṟcālikaḷukkuc ceṉṟu tāmtām ceyyum toḻilkaḷic ceyyat tuvaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. araciyal toḻil pārppavar tattam araciyal niliyaṅkaḷukkuc cellutaliyum, paḷḷik kūṭaṅkaḷil kalvi payilum ciṟuvarkaḷum, kallūrikaḷil, palkali payilum iḷiñarkaḷum tattam kaḻakaṅkaḷukku ēkutaliyum pāruṅkaḷ! kaṭavuḷ eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum tirukkōyilkaḷil ellām katavu tiṟantu kurukkaḷmār kāli vaḻipāṭu ceyvatu evvaḷavu ciṟanta makiḻcciyiṉit tarukiṉṟatu! iṅṅaṉamellām naṭipeṟum uyirkaḷiṉ ittaṉi muyaṟcikaḷukkum kāraṇamāvatu ellōṉatu tōṟṟamē eṉpatu naṉṟāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟataṉṟō?
itumaṭṭumō! namatu uyirvāḻkkikkuk, kaṭṭāyamāka vēṇṭappaṭum nel, kōtumi, payaṟu, paccikkāykaṟi mutaliyavikaḷi nām viḷittuk koḷḷutaṟkum veyilōṉatu veyiloḷiyē kāraṇamāyirukkiṉṟatu. veyil veḷiccam paṭāviṭṭāl entap puṟpūṇṭum, enta marañceṭi koṭiyum cevviyāka vaḷarntu payaṉ taramāṭṭā. iṉṉum, maḻikkāla, paṉikkālaṅkaḷil namatu uṭampu kuḷirāl viṟittup pōkāmalum, nām puḻaṅkum taṇṇīr paṉik kaṭṭiyāka iṟukip pōkāmal iruppataṟkum veyyōṉatu veppam mutaṉyika vēṇṭiyirukkiṟa taṉṟō?
nām irukkum iṭaṅkaḷellām paṉimūṭip pōkumāṉāl, nām entap paṇṭattiyāvatu payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷa muṭiyumā? namatuṭampu viṟittup pōkumāṉāl nām uyirōṭiruttal iyalumā? iyalātē? ātalāl, nām namatu uyir vāḻkkiyic cevvaṉē naṭipeṟuvittaṟkum, namatu vāḻkkikkuk kaṭṭāyamāka vēṇṭappaṭum ellāp paṇṭaṅkaḷum namakkup payaṉpaṭum niliyiliruntu utavu vataṟkum veyyōṉuṭiya oḷiyum veppamum mutaṉmiyāka iruttali nām karuttiṟ patittuk koḷvōmāka!
ivvāṟu, ellā uyirkaḷukkum aṟiviyum muyaṟciyiyum eḻupputalilum, avvuyirkaḷi uṭampukaḷil tōṉṟac ceytu avaṟṟip pātukāttalilum, avvuyirkaḷ nukarccikku vēṇṭiya ellā uṇavup poruḷkaḷiyum viḷittut tarutalilum pakalavaṉuṭiya taṉmiyum ceyalum kaṭavuḷiṉ taṉmiyiyum aruṭceyaliyum ottiruttalāl, appakalavaṉiyē kaṭavuḷāka allatu kaṭavuḷiṉ oḷi vaṭivākak karutik, kāliyil avaṉ kiḻakkil eḻumpōtum, māliyil avaṉ mēṟkil maṟiyum pōtum avaṉi uḷḷam urukik kikūppit toḻuvōmāka!
2. ulaka iyakkam
nām uṟiyum ivvulakamāṉatu vāṉ veḷiyil pantu pōl cuḻalum ōr uruṇṭi vaṭiviṉatākum. nām irukkum itaṉ mēṟparappiṉ miyattiliruntu itaṉi ūṭuruvit tuḷittuk koṇṭu aḷantapaṭiyē nām itaṉ kīḻuḷḷa mēṟparappiṉ miyattiṟ ceṉṟu cērak kūṭumāyiṉ, iḵtu 7,900 mil kuṟukkaḷavuḷḷatāy iruttali aṟintu koḷḷalām. itaṉ cuṟṟaḷavō 25,000 milākum.
iḵtu uruṇṭi vaṭiviṉateṉpatu eṅṅaṉam aṟiyak kūṭumeṉiṉ, nām oru kaṭaṟkariyiṟ ceṉṟu niṉṟu, akkariyi nōkki varum oru kappalik kāṇkuvamāyiṉ, neṭuntoliviliruntu varum akkappaliṉ mēluḷḷa pāymaraṅkaḷē mutaṉmutaṉ kaṭpulaṉākum. piṉṉar atu innilaccarivil paravi niṟkum kaṭalmēl ēṟik kiṭṭa varavarap pāymarattiṉ kīḻuḷḷa ataṉ muḻuvuṭampum piyappiya naṉku pulaṉāyt tōṉṟum. aṅṅaṉamē, nām niṟkum kariyiṉ tuṟimukattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭut toliviṟ cellum oru marakkalamāṉatu kaṭalnīr cariviṉ kīḻ iṟaṅka iṟaṅka ataṉ muḻuvuṭampum ciṟituciṟitāy maṟintupōka, ataṉ mēluḷḷa pāymaraṅkaḷē piṉṉum ciṟitu nēram variyil kaṭpulaṉā kiṉṟaṉa. piṉṉum piṉṉum atu kīḻ iṟaṅka ataṉ pāy maraṅkaḷum maṟintu pōkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl ivvulaka māṉatu uruṇṭi vaṭiviṉatātal peṟappaṭukiṉṟataṉṟō? iṉṉum namatu ilaṅkit tīviṉ teṉmuṉik kaṇṇatāṉa māttaḷi yiliruntu puṟappaṭṭuk kiḻakku nōkkiyē cellum oru nāvāyāṉatu, mīṇṭum ammāttaḷit tuṟimukat tilēyē vantu cērak kāṇṭalālum innilavulakam uruṇṭi vaṭiviṉateṉpatu viḷaṅkā niṟkiṉṟatu. itaṟku oru pantiṉ naṭuvil uḷḷa oru puḷḷiyiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu nērē cellum ōr eṟumpu tirumpavum appuḷḷiyilēyē vantu cērtali eṭuttuk kāṭṭalām. atuvēyumaṉṟit, tiṅkaḷ maṟivu (cantirakirakaṇa) kālattil, ataṉkaṇ kāṇappaṭum ivvulakattiṉ niḻalāṉatu vaṭṭavaṭiviṉatāyk kāṇappaṭuva tālum iḵtu uruṇṭi vaṭiviṉatātal teḷiyappaṭum.
avvāṟāyiṉ pantupōl uruṇṭirukkum innilamaṇṭilam vāṉveḷiyil ētoru paṟṟukkōṭum iṉṟic cuḻaṉṟu celvatu oru perum putumiyākat tōṉṟu
kiṉṟataṉṟō? eṉiṉ; ām; ārāyntu pārātavarkku atu putumitāṉ. ārāyntu pārppavarkkō, ataṉ uṇmi naṉku viḷaṅkum. ciṟumiyar iruvar etir etirē niṉṟu tamkikaḷik kōttup piṭittuk koṇṭu piṉṉē cāynta vaṇṇamāyt tumpi cuṟṟutalip pāruṅkaḷ! aṅṅaṉam cuṟṟuṅkāl piṉṉē cāyntirukkum avviruvaruṅ kīḻ viḻātatu ēṉ? avarkaḷ tam kikaḷi nīṭṭi iṟukap piṭittirut talālaṉṟō? atu pōlavē, pakalavaṉ maṇṭilamāṉatu innilavulakatti iḻukka, iḵtu ataṉi iḻukka, ivvāṟṟal oṉṟu maṟṟiyati viṭṭup piriyātāy vāṉveḷiyiṟ cuḻaṉṟu celkiṉṟateṉka. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, aṟivillāta ivviruvēṟu ulakum aṟivuṭiya ciṟumakārippōl oṉṟiyoṉṟu iḻuttuk koṇṭu cellutal yāṅṅaṉa meṉiṉ, aṟivuṭiya uyirkaḷ maṭṭumē oṉṟiyoṉṟu iḻuppaṉa, maṟṟiya iḻā eṉṟu koḷḷutal poruntātu. aṟivillāta poruḷkaḷum oṉṟiyoṉṟu iḻukka vallaṉavāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. oru kāntakkal taṉ parumaṉukku ēṟpac ciṟiya periya irumput tuṇṭukaḷi iḻuttal kāṇalām. akkāntak kalli oru kaṭitattiṉ kīḻ piṭittu, akkaṭitattiṉ mēl cila ūcikaḷi vittuk kīḻuḷḷa kāntak kalli neṭuka iḻuttāl mēluḷḷa irumpūcikaḷum, ataṉāl iḻukkap paṭṭu, akkaṭitattiṉ mēl nakarutalip pārkkalām. aṟivillāta kāntam iṅṅaṉam iḻukkum āṟṟal vāyntatāy iruttal pōlavē, aṟivillāta ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilamum, vāṉaveḷiyiṉūṭu innilavulakatti iḻukka, ituvum ataṉiyiḻukka iraṇṭum iṭiveḷiyiṟ cuḻaṉṟu celkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu ōrntukoḷka.
irākkālattil namakku nilavoḷiyiṉit tarum tiṅkaḷ maṇṭilamum innilavulakattāl iḻukkappaṭṭu, itaṉic cuṟṟiyapaṭiyē ñāyiṟṟiṉiyum cuṟṟic celkiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉamē, cevvāy, putaṉ, viyāḻaṉ, veḷḷi mutalāṉa ulakaṅkaḷum ñāyiṟṟiṉi iḻukka, atu maṟṟu ivvulakaṅkaḷ ellā vaṟṟiyum oruṅkē iḻukka, ivi tāmum tammuḷ oṉṟi yoṉṟu caṉṉal piṉṉalāy iḻukka ellām vāṉ veḷiyiṟ paṟpala pantukaḷ pōl ñāyiṟṟiṉic cūḻntu cellā niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
aṟṟēl, maṟṟi āṟu maṇṭilaṅkaḷum ñāyiṟṟiṉic cūḻntu celkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu koḷḷutal eṉṉiyeṉil; ivvēḻu ulakaṅkaḷilum, ñāyiṟu oṉṟumē mikapperiyatu. nāmirukkum innilavulakattiviṭa ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilam paṉṉiraṇṭu nūṟāyiram maṭaṅku periyatāy irukkiṉṟatu. ataṟku aṭuttapaṭiyākap periyatāy uḷḷatu viyāḻa maṇṭilamē ākum; ivviyāḻaṉ namatu maṇṇulakattiṉum ōrāyirattu nāṉūṟumaṭaṅku periyatu. itaṟku aṭuttapaṭi periyatu caṉi maṇṭilam. itaṟkuc ciṟiyatu namatu maṇṇulaku. namatu maṇṇulakukkum ciṟiyatu veḷḷiyulaku. ataṟkum ciṟiyatu cevvāy ulaku. ataṟkum ciṟiyatu putaṉulakākum. iṅṅaṉam maṟṟi āṟum, pakal maṇṭilattiṟkuc ciṟiyaṉavāy iruttalāl, ciṟiyavākiya avi periyatākiya pakalulakiṉāl īrkkappaṭṭu ataṉic cūḻntu ōṭiyapaṭiyāy ulavukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟuṇarntu koḷka.
iṉi, murukappirāṉukku nērttikkaṭaṉ celuttum toṇṭar cilar, avaṉ eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum tirukkōyilic cuṟṟi nilattiṟkiṭantu puraṇṭukoṇṭē valam varutali nīṅkaḷ pārttirukkalām. aṅṅaṉam puraṇṭu valam varupavar mutalil tammittām cuṟṟip piṉṉar akkōyilic cuṟṟi valam varutal pōlavē, ivvulakamum mutalil taṉṉit tāṉ cuṟṟip piṟaku ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattiṉic cuṟṟi varukiṉṟatu.
iḵtu orumuṟi taṉṉittāṉ cuṟṟutalukku irupattu nāṉku maṇi nēram piṭikkiṉṟatu. mēṟkiliruntu kiḻakku mukamāy iḵtu iṅṅaṉam taṉṉittāṉ cuṟṟuṅkāl ñāyiṟṟiṉi nōkki iyaṅkum itaṉ orupāti pakal veḷiccam uṭiyatākavum, ñāyiṟṟiṉ mukamāyt tirumpāta ataṉ maṟupāti iruṇṭa allatu nilavoḷi keḻumiya irāppoḻutu uṭiyatāyum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nām uṟiyum itteṉtamiḻnāṭṭil pakalavaṉ ucciyil viḷaṅkum vēḷi naṇpakaṟ poḻutāyirukkiyil, namakkunērē kīḻ uḷḷa amerikkā tēyam naḷḷirāp poḻutu vāyntatāy irukkiṉṟatu; namatu tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkum amerikkā tēyattiṟkum naṭuvil uḷḷatākiya iṅkilāntu tēyamō ap
pōtu viṭiyaṟkālam uṭiyatāyt tikaḻkiṉṟatu.
ivvāṟāka ivvulakam irupattunāṉku maṇinērattil taṉṉittāṉ cuṟṟiyapaṭiyāy, taṉṉilum paṉṉūṟāyira maṭaṅku peritāṉa pakalavaṉ maṇṭilatti muṟṟuñ cuṟṟi varuvataṟku munnūṟṟu aṟupattintēkāl nāṭkaḷ ākiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam itu orumuṟi katiravaṉic cuṟṟi varuṅkālamē ōr āṇṭu (vaṭamoḻiyil varuṣam) eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu. aṅṅaṉam veyyavaṉ ulakiṉic cuṟṟic cuḻaṉṟu varutal uṇmiyāyiṉ, itaṉ iyakkam namakkup pulaṉākāmiyum, pakalavaṉē itaṉic cuṟṟi varupavaṉākap pulappaṭutalum eṉṉiyeṉiṉ; iḵtu oru maṇinērattil āyiram mil viḻukkāṭu katiravaṉ maṇṭilatti miku virivākac cūḻntu cuḻalutalāl, attuṇi virivākac cellum itaṉkaṇ uyirvāḻum namakku itaṉ iyakkam pulaṉākātu pōka, itaṉāṟ cuṟṟappaṭum pakalavaṉē itaṉic cuṟṟi varupavaṉpōl pulappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. oru maṇikku muppatu mil viḻukkāṭu ōṭum pukivaṇṭiyil cellum nammaṉōrkkē appuki vaṇṭiyiṉ ōṭṭam pulappaṭāmal ataṉ irupakkattum niṟkum nilaṉum, marañceṭi koṭikaḷumē ōṭuvatu pōl pulappaṭumāyiṉ, niṉittaṟkum ariya kaṭuvirivuṭaṉ orumaṇi nērattiṟku āyirammil viḻukkāṭu cuḻaṉṟōṭum innilavulakiṉ iyakkam namakkut teṉpaṭumō colluṅkaḷ! ākavē, niliyil uḷḷatu pōl kāṇap paṭuvatu paṟṟi immaṇṇulaku nilam eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻaṅkappaṭiṉum, ārāyntu pārkkuṅkāl vāṉakkaṭalil itu mikutiyum virintu ōṭum ōr ōṭamākavē kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu eṉka.
iṉi, innilavulakattiṉ iyakkattil vēṟoru mutaṉmiyāṉa taṉmiyum uḷatu. itu teṟku vaṭakkil ceṅkuttāy niṉṟapaṭi cuḻalāmal taṉ parumaṉ 360 paṅkil ciṟitēṟakkuṟiyap patiṉintu paṅku cāyntapaṭiyāy ñāyiṟṟiṉic cuṟṟic cuḻaṉṟu celkiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam cāyntu cuḻalvataṉāl itaṉ vaṭamuṉi teṉmuṉikaḷ āṟu tiṅkaḷ pakalavaṉ mukamāy tirumpiyum piṉ āṟu tiṅkaḷ pakalavaṉi viṭṭut tirumpiyum cuḻalkiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl avviru muṉikaḷil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷ āṟu tiṅkaḷ oḷiyum piṉ āṟu tiṅkaḷ iruḷum vāyntaṉavāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamuṉiyic cārnta nāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇatāṉa mērumaliyic cūḻnta pakutikaḷil āṟu tiṅkaḷ katiravaṉ oḷiyum, maṟṟi āṟu tiṅkaḷ avaṉ aṅkuk kāṇappaṭāmiyiṉ iruḷum toṭarpāy irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu māpārata vaṉaparvam (163, 37, 38) pakarvatum namatu nilavulakiṉ cāynta iyakkattāl avviru muṉikaḷil kāṇappaṭum avviruvaki nikaḻccikaḷiyē kuṟippikkiṉṟatu. ātaliṉāl tāṉ, vaṭamuṉiyil uṟiyum makkaḷukku āṟu tiṅkaḷ oru pakaṟpoḻutāyum, maṟṟi āṟu tiṅkaḷ ōr irāppoḻutāyum iṅkuḷḷavarāl koḷḷap paṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭatēyaṅkaḷil uṟiyum makkaḷit tēvarkaḷākak koḷḷutal itteṉṉāṭṭavarkku vaḻakkam. ātalāl, pakaloḷi vīcum āṟu tiṅkaḷiyum, attēvarkaṭku oru pakaṟ poḻutākavum, avvoḷiyiṉṟi iruḷcūḻnta ēṉi āṟu tiṅkaḷiyum avarkaṭku ōr irāppoḻutākavum innāṭṭavar paṇṭutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. innuṭpaṅkaḷ ellām paṇṭi āriyamakkaḷiṉ paḻiya uṟiyuḷi naṉkārāynta pālakaṅkātara tilakarāl virivāka eṭuttu viḷakkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamuṉiyil uḷḷārkku ovvōrāṇṭilum pakalavaṉoḷi mutaṉmutal kaṭpulaṉāy tōṉṟum viṭiyaṟkālam paṅkuṉit tiṅkaḷ oṉpatām nāḷ tuvaṅkukiṉṟatu. atiliruntu āṟu tiṅkaḷ variyil cuṭaravaṉ oḷi vāṉiṉ kaṇ tulaṅkiya paṭiyāy iruntu puraṭṭācit tiṅkaḷ eṭṭām nāḷ maṟintu pōka, atiliruntu maṟu paṅkuṉi variyil avvaṭanāṭukaḷ iruḷ cūḻap paṭṭaṉavāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. aṅṅaṉam iruḷ cūḻntiruntālum, pakalavaṉ tōṉṟuvataṟku muṉ aṟikuṟiyākak kāṇappaṭum vikaṟip poḻutu paṅkuṉikku mūṉṟu tiṅkaḷ muṉṉarē mārkaḻit tuvakkattil annāṭṭavar kaṇkaṭkup pulaṉākutalāltāṉitteṉṉāṭṭavarkaḷ tēvarkaṭkup poḻutu viṭiyum kālamāka mārkaḻit tiṅkaḷik koṇṭāṭi varukiṉṟārkaḷ. āṟu tiṅkaḷ iruḷil kiṭantu mūḻkum makkaṭku oru vakiyil āṟutalum makiḻcciyum tarum poruṭṭu, iṟivaṉ avvaṭamuṉi nāṭukaḷukku oru peru miṉṉal oḷiyiṉic ciṟitukālam variyil vāṉiṉkaṇ tōṟṟuvittu varutaliyum ciṟārkaḷ karuttiṟ patittu, iṟivaṉatu pēraruḷ tiṟatti viyantu vāḻttuvārāka!
3. kuṟiñci-mali
nām uyirvāḻum inta nila ulakamāṉatu, maliyum, kaṭalum, kāṭum, nāṭum eṉa nāṉku pakutikaḷāka nammuṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil pirikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. innāṉkum allāta veṟu maṇalveḷiyum uṇṭu. maliyum malicārnta iṭamum kuṟiñciyeṉṟu collappaṭum.
mali eṉṉum collukkup poruḷ valimiyiṉi uṭiyatu. mal eṉṟāl valimi. malikaḷellām perumpālum valivāṉa karuṅkallāl ākkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. mikavum uyaramāka irukkum maliyi ōṅkal, piṟaṅkal, poruppu, veṟpu, eṉṟum, ōriṭattil kuṟukkē vaḷarntu nīṇṭu kiṭakkum maliyi vilaṅkal eṉṟum, oṉṟaṉ mēloṉṟu aṭukkuaṭukkāy vaḷarntirukkum maliyi aṭukkal eṉṟum, etiroli ceyyum maliyic cilampu eṉṟum, mūṅkiṟkāṭukaḷ uḷḷa maliyi vari eṉṟum, kāṭukaḷ aṭarnta maliyi iṟumpu eṉṟum, ciṟiya maliyik kuṉṟu, kuvaṭu, kuṟumpoṟi eṉṟum, maṇ mikunta maliyip poṟṟi, pocci eṉṟum, malip pakkattic cāral eṉṟum paḻiya tamiḻnūlkaḷ kūṟā niṟkum.
malikaḷiṉ iyaṟki aḻakik kāṇavēṇṭumāṉāl teṟkē tirukkuṟṟālam, pāvanācam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa malikaḷiyum kiḻakkē kīḻkkaṇavāy malit toṭaril uḷḷa cērvarāyaṉ maliyiyum, naṭuvē nīlakiri maliyiyum, mēṟkē puṉalūr pakkamāy uḷḷa maliyāḷattiyum, vaṭakkē imayamaliyiyum pōyp pārttal vēṇṭum.
teṟkēyuḷḷa pāvanāca maliyaṭivārattil taṉiniṟkum civapirāṉ tirukkōyilum, ataṉ valappuṟattum etirilum ōṭivarum poruniyāṟum (tāmiraparuṇi) avvāṟṟaṅ
kariyil vāṉ aḷāvi niṟkum maraṅkaḷum, avvāṟṟaṅkariyiṉ appakkattē neṭukattōṉṟum malittoṭariṉ ōriṭattil mukaṭu civanta ōṭukaḷ vēyntu uṭampellām veṇmiyāka amikkappaṭṭut tōṉṟum pañcālik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum, kōyiliṉ piṉpuṟattē avvāṟṟiṉkaṇ kāṇappaṭum maṇṭapaṅkaḷum, ammalit
toṭar eṅkum uḷḷa aṭarnta kāṭukaḷum putitu ceṉṟu pārppavarkaḷukku avar muṉṉaṟiyāta ōr iṉpavuṇarviṉi ūṭṭi avaruḷḷattik kiḷarac ceykiṉṟaṉa.
vikācit tiṅkaḷ muṭivil aṅkēceṉṟu, attirukkōyililiruntu oru nāḻiki vaḻi naṭantu ceṉṟāl, akattiyar kōyiloṉṟu kāṇappaṭum. malip pakkatti
liruntu virinta veḷḷāṭi toṅka viṭṭāṟ pōlak kumukumu eṉṟa ōciyuṭaṉ kīḻviḻum aruviyiṉ kāṭci ettakiyavar uḷḷattiyum kavarum taṉmiyatāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. avvaruvinīr kīḻviḻum iṭattil mika āḻntatoru maṭu amintuḷḷatu. ammaṭuvukkuc cellum ōr oṟṟiyaṭippāti ceṅkuttāṉa maliccuvari aṭuttup pōkiṉṟatu. appātiyil celluṅkāl, kīḻ iṟaṅkum aruvi nīrt tivaliyum cāraṟkāṟṟum celvōriṉ mukattilum uṭampilum etirttu aṟivatu pōṟpaṭum; appōtu kaṇviḻittup pārttālum varuttamāyirukkum. eṉṟālum, ataṟku añcātu etirēṟic ceṉṟu, ammaṭuvil mikavum viḻippāy iṟaṅki muḻukiṉāl, mikak kuḷirnta annīriṉ muḻukku mikkatoru maṉakkaḷippiṉiyum āṟutaliyum tarum; uṭampiṉ nalattiṟkum mika ēṟṟatu.
avvaḷavu naṭantu ceṉṟu, ammaṭuvil muḻuka iyalātavarkaḷ, ammaṭuvil niṟintu vaḻintu, mēṟcoṉṉa akattiyar kōyil pakkamāy virintu ōṭivarum poruni
yāṟṟilēyē nīrāṭalām. āṉāl, avviṭattil avvāṟu āḻam iṉṟi iṭuppaḷaviṟkum kuṟinta nīr uṭiyatāy ēkutalāl, atil paṭuttapaṭiyāyiruntu muḻukutalē kūṭum. aṅṅaṉam muḻukuṅkāl viḻippāyirukka vēṇṭum. ēṉeṉil avvāṟṟiṉ nīr malimēliruntu kīḻviḻuntu miku virivāy celvataṉāl, āḷi nilikkaviṭāmal puraṭṭit taḷḷik koṇṭu pōyviṭum. mēlum, avyāṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭi yeṅkum oruperum pūcaṉikkāy parumaṉ uḷḷa uruṇṭik kaṟkaḷ niṟintirukkiṉṟaṉa! ataṉāl, ataṉ aṭiyiṟ kālippatiyavittu niṟṟalum muṭiyātu; ciṟitu picaki viḻuntālum puraṇṭālum maṇṭi aṭipaṭum; uṭampum kālkikaḷum avaṟṟil uricik kāyappaṭum. āṉālum, viḻippāyiruntu nīrāṭupavarkkup paḷiṅkupōl teḷintu ōṭum ataṉnīr mēṉimēṟ kuḷukuḷuveṉṟupaṭṭup pēriṉimi yiṉit tarum.
nīrāṭiyapiṉ avvāṟṟaṅ kariyiṉiṭattē irukkum akattiyar kōyilil ceṉṟu iṟivaṉi vaṇaṅkuvatu uḷḷattip pēr aruḷ iṉpattil amiḻttum. cuṟṟi vāṉōṅkiya malikaḷum kāṭukaḷum aṭarntirukka, etirē uḷḷatoru mali mukaṭṭiliruntu pāl oḻukuvatu pōl ōveṉṟa iriccaluṭaṉ aruvi nīr viḻa, viḻunta annīr tiraṇṭa kaṟkaḷiṉ mēl virintōṭic cella, aṅṅaṉam annīr cellum poruni āṟṟaṅkarimēl avakattiyar kōyil taṉimiyil niṟka. ataṉuḷ oru kurukkaḷ vaḻipāṭu ceyyumpōtu, aṅkē vaṇaṅkac celvōr maṉam, ulaka niṉivukaḷi viṭṭu, aṅkuḷḷa iyaṟkik kāṭcikaḷiṉ aḻakāl mēl untappaṭṭu, uyirkaḷiṉ taṉitta tuṇiyaṟṟa nilimiyiṉiyum, annilimiyil iṟivaṉ oruvaṉē avvuyirkaṭku oppaṟṟa tuṇivaṉāy vāyttuḷḷa arumiyiṉiyum niṉintu niṉintu nekkuviṭṭu urukukiṉṟatu.
ippāvanācak kāṭciyiṉi oppavē, tirukkuṟṟāla mali aṭivārattilum periyatoru paḻiya civapirāṉ tirukkōyil amintuḷḷatu. ataṉ arukēyum, mali
mēliruntu viḻum periyatōr aruvi vīḻcci kāṇṭaṟku mika iṉiyatōr aḻakiṉi uṭittāyt tikaḻkiṉṟatu. vēṉiṟ kāla naṭuvil ivvaruviyil talimuḻukutaṟ poruṭṭup pallāyirakkaṇakkiṉarāṉa makkaḷ itteṉṉāṭu eṅkaṇu miruntu vantu tiraḷkiṉṟaṉar.
ittirukkuṟṟāla maliyiṉ aṭivārattiliruntu mēlē cellutaṟkut tiruttamāṉa akaṉṟatorupāti amikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. itu vaḷintu mēlē caṇpakāṭavi variyil celkiṉṟatu. itaṉūṭu celkiyil itaṉ irupuṟattum ōṅki vaḷarntirukkum aṭarnta marakkāṭukaḷiṉ kāṭci maṉattiṟku accattōṭu kalanta oru makiḻcciyiṉit tōṟṟuvikkum. caṇpakāṭaviyiṉ arukē aḻakiyatoru maṇṭapam amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu; aṅkē aruvi viḻum iṭattiṉ pakkattē oru kukiyum uḷatu; irupatu āṇṭu
kaḷukkumuṉ yām aṅkē ceṉṟapōtu iḷamparuvattiṉarāṉa oru tuṟavi akkukiyil taṅkiyiruntaṉar. emmik kaṇṭatum mukam malarntu varavēṟṟut, tām irukkum akkukiyiṉ uḷḷē emmi aḻittu, ataṉ uḷ amippi emakkuk kāṭṭiṉār.
iruperuṅ kaṟpāṟikaḷ oṉṟiyoṉṟu toṭutalāl aḵtu oru ciṟiya aṟipōl iyaṟkiyamippu vāyntiruntatu. ataṉuḷ uyaramāṉa oruvar nērāka niṟṟal iyalātu. kuṉintapaṭiyākac ceṉṟutāṉ irukkavēṇṭum. nāṉku pēr irukkavum, iruvar paṭukkavum pōtumāṉa iṭantāṉ uḷatu. attuṟavi akkuki vāyilil ciṟu ceṅkal cuvar eḻuppi atil oru ciṟu katavu porutti iruntār. veḷikkāṟṟu uḷḷē nuḻivataṟkāka akkataviṉ mēṟ pakutiyil ciṟuciṟutuḷikaḷ uṇṭu. irākkālattil aruvi nīr kuṭikkavarum yāṉi, puli, ciṟutti, kāṭṭuppaṉṟi mutalāṉa vilaṅkukaḷ urumum ōciyum avi oṉṟi yoṉṟu ciṉantu tākkic ceyyum iriccalum perun tikili viḷikkumeṉṟum, nilākkālattil akkataviṉ tuḷivaḻiyē tām avvilaṅkukaḷiṉ vaṭivattip pārppatu uṇṭeṉṟum avar emakkuk kūṟiṉār. kīḻē maliyaṭi vārattil viḻum aruviyip pārkkilum mēlē caṇpakāṭaviyil viḻum aruvinīr kuḷirntatāy irukkiṉṟatu; taṇṇīriṉ aḷavum ciṟitu kuṟintatāka iruttalāl talimēlum uṭampiṉ mēlum ataṉit tāṅki muḻukuvatu iṅkē eḷitāyum mika iṉitāyum irukkum.
iṉic caṇpakāṭaviyit tāṇṭi, iṉṉum mēlēṟi uyarattiluḷḷa tēṉaruvikkuc cellat tiruttamāṉa akaṉṟa pāti illi. iṇṭu iṭukkut teriyāmal aṭarn
tirukkum peruṅkāṭukaḷiṉ ūṭē ōr oṟṟiyaṭip pātitāṉ celkiṉṟatu. appāti neṭuka utirnta ilic cettikaḷum, vērkaḷum, koṭikaḷum nirampiyiruttalāl avaṟṟiṉmēl naṭappatu accantarum; iṭiyiṭiyē ciṟuciṟu malippiḷavukaḷum uṇṭu; āṉālum, avikaḷi eḷitil tāṇṭic cellalām. tiṭīreṉṟu, yāṉi, vēṅkip puli, muḷḷampaṉṟi mutalāṉa maṟavilaṅkukaḷ vantu kuṟukkiṭutal uṇṭākiyāl, mēlē tēṉaruvikkuc celvōr oruvar iruvarāy pōtal kūṭātu. aintāṟu pērkkuk kuṟiyāmaṟ kiyil taṭikkampu tāṅki iriccal iṭṭukkoṇṭē pakal vēḷiyiṟ cellavēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ceṉṟāl tēṉaruviyip pōy aṭiyalām. aṭintatum aṅkuḷḷa kāṭci kaṇṇum maṉamum kavarntu mēl ōṅki viḷaṅkutal aṟiyappaṭum. aṅkē viḻum tēṉaruviyāṉatu mikavum ceṅkuttāṉa oru malic cuvariṉ mēliruntu vaḻikiṉṟatu. av varuvic cuvaruṭaṉ valappuṟattil iṇintirukkum kaṟpāṟi, mēlē kuṭikavittāṟ pōl amintuḷḷatu; mēliruntu tēṉaruvi vaḻiyum mūliyil nālintu teṉṉimara uyarattil vaṭṭamāṉa oru peruntēṉaṭi iruntatu. avvaḷavu periya tēṉ aṭiyi yām vēṟu eṅkumē kaṇṭatilli. ataṉ kuṟukkaḷavu ciṟitu ēṟakkuṟiyap pattu aṭi irukkum. ataṉ niṟañ ciṟu civappāy iruntamiyāl, atu kāliyil eḻum pakalavaṉ vaṭivi ottiruntatu. attēṉaṭiyiṉ pakkattuḷḷa mūli mēliruntu aruvinīr vīḻtal paṟṟiyē ataṉit tēṉaruviyeṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟum, ek kālattumē attēṉaṭi aṅku irukkum eṉṟum emmuṭaṉ vantār collak kēṭṭōm.
tēṉaruvi kīḻviḻum iṭam āḻnta oru ciṟu kuṭṭamāyirukkiṟatu. mēliruntu viḻum nīriṉ kaṭumiyāl akkuṭṭam nuriyum kumiḻiyum uṭiyatāy ōyātu niṟintu taṉṉiliruntu maṟṟoru ciṟṟaruviyiṉi uṇṭākka, acciṟṟaruviyē varavarap peritākik, kīḻē caṇpakāṭaviyilum, ataṟkum kīḻē tirukkuṟṟālap perumāṉ kōyiliṉ pakkattum pōy vīḻkiṉṟatu. tēṉaruviyiṉ kīḻ uḷḷa kuṭṭam oru kaṟpāṟiyiṉ kuṭiyil amintiruttalāl, ataṉkaṇ nirampum nīr mikka teḷivuṭiyatāy tūytāy mikak kuḷirntu irukkiṉṟatu.
4. mulli-kāṭu
maraṅkaḷ aṭarnta nilappakutiyē perumpālum kāṭu eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. kāṭum kāṭu cārnta iṭamum mulliyeṉap peyar peṟumeṉṟu tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟāniṟkum. paruttuyarnta maraṅkaḷ neruṅkiya kāṭṭi valli eṉṟum, ciṟu maraṅkaḷ miṭinta kāṭṭi iṟumpu, kuṟuṅkāṭu eṉṟum, ciṟu tūṟukaḷ pampiya kāṭṭi aril, aṟal, patukki eṉṟum mika mutirntu muṟṟippōṉa maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya kāṭṭi muti eṉṟum, maraṅkaḷ karintu pōṉa kāṭṭip pocci, curam, poti eṉṟum aracaṉatu kāvaliluḷḷa kāṭṭik kaṇiyam, miḷi, araṇ eṉṟum paṇṭutoṭṭut tamiḻmakkaḷ vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam palavakiyāl vaḻaṅkappaṭum kāṭukaḷil perumpālāṉa malikaḷiṉ mēlum malikaḷiṉ kīḻum malicārnta iṭaṅkaḷilumē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ciṟupāṉmiya kaṭal cārnta iṭaṅkaḷil irukkiṉṟaṉa. tirumaṟikkāṭu (vētāraṇiyam) eṉṉum civapirāṉ tirukkōyil uḷḷa ūriyaṭutta kāṭu kaṭalaṭutta nilattiṉkaṇ uḷḷatu. paṉaṅkāṭu uṭivēlaṅkāṭu mutaliyaṉa pāṇṭināṭṭil irāmēcuvaram, tiruccentūr mutaliyavikaṭku aṇmiyavāṉa kaṭalcārnta iṭaṅkaḷil mikutiyāy uḷḷaṉa.
vāṉ aḷāviya maraṅkaḷ aṭarntu, toliviliruntu nōkkuvārkkup pacuntaḻip pōrviyuṭaṉ tōṉṟi, avartam kaṇṇiyum karuttiyum kavarvaṉavāy, arukē ceṉṟu tammuḷ nuḻintu nōkkuvārkkup palvēṟu vakiyāṉa paruttuyarnta maraṅkaḷum ceṭikoṭikaḷum vāyntaṉavāy, uḷḷē cellaccella iruṇṭa iṭaṅ
kaḷum iṭiyiṭiyē pakalavaṉ veḷiccampaṭum cantuveḷikaḷum tam akattu uṭiyaṉavāy, nīr ūṟṟukaḷum ciṟṟaruvikaḷum āṅkāṅku amiyappeṟṟu avaṟṟi aṭuttuḷḷa marañceṭi koṭikaḷil palavakip paṟavik kūṭṭaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ olikaḷum nirampap peṟṟavaṉavāy tammuḷ celvāriṉ uḷḷatti accuṟuttum yāṉi puli karaṭi perumpāmpukaḷiṉ aṭiccuvaṭukaḷum uṭaṟpativukaḷum poruntiṉavāy, carukukaḷ pūkkaḷ kaṉikaḷ vittukaḷ iṭaṅkaḷ tōṟum utirap peṟṟaṉavāy, iṭi
yiṭiyē paṭṭu vīḻntu uḷ iyaṅkuvāriṉ vaḻikaḷi maṟikkum ularnta maraṅkaḷum kōṭukaḷum vaḷārkaḷum muṭkaḷum vērkaḷum koṭikaḷum tutintaṉavāy uḷḷa peruṅkāṭukaḷip puṉalūr, nīlamali, tirukkuṟṟālamali, aḻakarmali, pāvanācamali, veḷḷimali, marutamali, cērvarāyaṉmali, imayamali mutalāṉa malip pāṅku
kaḷukku yām ceṉṟa kālaṅkaḷil kaṇṭu, muṉṉaṟiyāta oru perumakiḻcciyum iṟumpūtum eytiṉōm. ittakiya peruṅkāṭukaḷip pārātavarkaḷukku, ōraṟivu uyirkaḷiṉ amippil iṟivaṉ tōṟṟuvittirukkum vaṉappum uyarnta kāṭciyum viyappum payaṉum oru ciṟitumē viḷaṅkā.
kāṉakattiṉ kaviṉik kāṉakattilaṉṟi veṟeṅkum kāṇal iyalātu. ettaṉi vaṭivāṉa ilikaḷ! ettaṉi niṟamāṉa malarkaḷ! ettaṉi cuviyāṉa kāykaḷ! ettaṉi iṉippāṉa paḻaṅkaḷ! ettaṉi vakiyāṉa maraṅkaḷ ceṭikaḷ koṭikaḷ! ammamma! iṟivaṉ paṭippiṉ palvēṟu vakiyāṉa viyattaku amippum, aḻakum oruṅku potuḷiya aṭavikaḷiṉ viḻumiya taṉmiyi eṅṅaṉam kūṟuvēm! evvāṟu kāṭṭuvēm! kāṉakaṅkaḷiṉ aṭarntuyarnta tōṟṟattik kāṇkiyil uḷḷamum uṇarvum mēluyarntu celkiṉṟaṉa! viyappum putumiyum karuttik kavarnteḻukiṉṟaṉa!
avi maṭṭumō! niṉṟa niliyil niṟkum marañ ceṭi koṭikaḷākiya ōraṟivuyirkaḷ, iṭam viṭṭup peyarum ēṉi uyirkaḷukku ettaṉi vakiyiṟ payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa! nīrum niḻalum uṇavum tūya kāṟṟum avikaḷ aṉittuyirkaṭkum aḷittu varukiṉṟaṉa kāṇmiṉkaḷ! kāṭukaḷ illiyāyiṉ, veyyavaṉ cuṭukatirkaḷāl vetuppappaṭum innilam nīrppaciyaṟa vaṟaṇṭu viṭum; āviyāka māṟi vāṉiṉkaṇ ceṉṟa nīr kuḷirkāṟṟu vīcutaṟku vaḻiyiṉmiyāl tirumpa maḻiyākak kīḻ iṟaṅkātu. ākavē, uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷit tarum maram ceṭi koṭikaḷum ēṉip payirkaḷum uṇṭākā. ivvuṇmi, kāṭukaḷi aḻittuviṭum iṭaṅkaḷil maḻi arukip pōtalāl naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
uṇavē aṉṟi, namatuyir vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta tūya kāṟṟum, kāṭukaḷil tuvaṉṟiya marañceṭi koṭikaḷiṉālēyē nām varappeṟukiṉṟōm. ivōraṟi
vuyirttokutikaḷ mikuntillāmal, ēṉi uyirttokutikaḷē eṅkum mikuntirukkumāyiṉ ivi veḷiviṭum naccukkāṟṟi ivvuyirkaḷē mīṇṭum mīṇṭum uṭ
koṇṭu, ataṉāl nōyppaṭṭuc cila nāṭkaḷil iṟantoḻiyum. maṟṟuk kāṉakaṅkaḷilum piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum uḷḷa maram ceṭi koṭikaḷ payirkaḷō īraṟivu mutal āṟaṟivu īṟāka uṭiya ūrvaṉa, nīr vāḻvaṉa, paṟappaṉa, naṭappaṉavākiya ciṟṟuyirkaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, vilaṅkukaḷ, makkaḷ mutalāṉa iyaṅkumuyirkaḷ veḷiviṭum naccuk kāṟṟi uḷḷiḻuttu, ataṉ naccut taṉmiyi nīkkit tūya kāṟṟi veḷiviṭṭu ataṉi eṅkum paravac ceykiṉṟaṉa. ivvuṇmi, payir paccikaḷ illāta nakaraṅkaḷil iruppavarkaḷ aṭuttaṭuttu nōykaḷāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭu virivil māyntu pōtalālum, maramceṭi koṭikaḷ niṟinta nāṭṭuppuṟaṅkaḷil iruppavarkaḷ nōyillā yākkiyiṉarāy nīṇṭakālam naṉku uyir vāḻtalālum iṉitaṟiyappaṭum.
iṉi,  ulakattilē mikapperiya kāṭukaḷ, vaṭa amerikkap peruntēyattiṉ oru pakutiyākiya kaṉaṭā nāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇētāṉ irukkiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa kāṭukaḷiṉ aḷavu munnūṟṟu eḻupattintu kōṭi kāṇikaḷākumeṉṟu kaṇakkeṭuttirukkiṟārkaḷ. teṉṉamerikka tēyattilum, ittuṇip periya kāṭukaḷ iruntālum, avikaḷil maraṅkaḷ mika aṭarntiruttalālum, koṭiya kāṭṭuvilaṅkukaḷum naccup perumpāmpukaḷum mikuntiruttalālum avaṟṟiṉuḷḷē makkaḷ nuḻintu ceṉṟu pārttalum avaṟṟi aḷaveṭuttalum iyalavilliyeṉṟu ārāyntupōyk kāṇum āciriyarkaḷ aṟivikkiṉṟārkaḷ. airōppiyap peruntēyattiṉ vaṭapakutiyil uḷatākiya uruciyā tēyattilum mikapperiya kāṭukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ aḷavum ciṟitēṟakkuṟiya munnūṟṟeḻupattu aintu kōṭi kāṇikaḷākumeṉṟum it
tuṟiyil valla aṟiñar kaṇakkeṭutturikkiṉṟaṉar.
iṉi uyarattilum parumaṉilum kuṟinta aḷaviṉavāṉa maraṅkaḷ niṟinta kuṟuṅkāṭukaḷ perumpālum ellā nāṭukaḷilum āṅkāṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tirumaṟik kāṭṭiṟkum kōṭiyakkarikkum iṭiyiluḷḷa kāṭṭik kuṟuṅkāṭu eṉṟē kūṟutal vēṇṭum. irāmēcuvarattiṟkum pāmpaṉukkum iṭiyiluḷḷa kāṭum kuṟuṅkāṭēyākum. tiruccentūriyaṭutta neytal nilap pakutikaḷiluḷḷa paṉaṅkāṭu uṭivēlaṅkāṭukaḷum kuṟuṅkāṭṭil cērntaṉavēyākum.
iṉic, ciṟu tūṟukaḷum kuṟuñceṭikaḷum pampiya kāṭukaḷum uṇṭu. yāmirukkum ippallavapurattiṟku arukil tūṟukaḷum ciṟu ceṭikaḷum niṟinta oru nīṇ
ṭakaṉṟa kāṭu uḷatu. iṉṉōraṉṉa ciṟu kāṭukaḷ, mēṟkūṟiya peruṅkuṟuṅ kāṭukaḷip pōl kāṭcikku iṉiyaṉavāy illāviṭiṉum, nilattiṉ vaṟaṭciyit taṇittu, ēḻi eḷiyōrkaḷ viṟakuveṭṭi viṟṟup piḻippataṟkup peritum utaviyākiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, mika mutirntu muṟṟippōṉamiyāl aṭarntillāmal alicalāyirukkum ilikaḷiyum, avvilikaḷ tāmum illāmal veṟuṅkōṭukaḷ kompu
kaḷiyum uṭiya periya maraṅkaḷ niṟinta kāṭṭiṉ pakutikaḷik kōyamputtūrkkaṭutta veḷḷimalip peruṅkāṭṭiṉ iṭiyiṭiyē kaṇṭu vantōm. taḻinta paciyamarat tokutikaḷik kaṇṭukaḷitta kaṇkaḷukku, vaṟṟalāy niṟkum immaraṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam varuttatti viḷivittatu. celvattiṉ ceḻumiyāl kiḷiñarum naṇparum cūḻa vāḻntu vantavar oru cilar ciṟitu kālattil accelvatti yiḻantu, cuṟṟattār evarumiṉṟi, uṭampiṉ oḷiyum uḷḷak kiḷarcciyum aṟṟut taṉiniṟṟal pōlavum, iḷamiyum aḻakum tulaṅkat talimiyil vīṟi niṉṟār, piṟar cilar avviḷamiyum aḻakum talimiyum māṟi vaṟṟiya yākkiyiṉarāy varunti niṟṟal pōlavum, pacceṉṟa potuḷiya iḷantaḻikaḷ utirntu puṭkaḷum avvilaṅ kiṉaṅkaḷum aṇukap peṟāmal nīrppaciyaṟṟu vaḷārkaḷākiya paḻutta talimayirkaḷ kāṟṟil aliyak kōṭukaḷākiya kikaḷi nīṭṭi virittut tamatu vāṭṭattik kuṟippavaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭṭa ammaraṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, uyarnili virivil māṟappeṟum makkaḷiṉ ivvulakavāḻviṉ nilimiyik kuṟippatoṉṟākavē emakkut tōṉṟiyatu.
iṉik, karinta maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya poccikkāṭukaḷ katiravaṉ veppattāl kaṉaṉṟu koṇṭirukkum maṇal veḷikaḷākiya pāli nilaṅkaḷilētām arumiyāyk kāṇappaṭum.
iṉi, mutaliṟ kūṟiya peruṅkāṭukaḷellām, perumpālum āṅkāṅku aracu celuttum aracarkaḷiṉ kāvalil vikkappaṭṭu, avarkaṭkup pala vakiyil ērāḷamāṉa varuvāyit tantu varukiṉṟaṉa. kāṭukaḷil mikutiyāka vaḷarum tēkku, pāli, mutiri, āccā mutalāṉa maraṅkaḷ vīṭukaḷ, toḻiṟcālikaḷ, kappalkaḷ, paṭakukaḷ mutaliyaṉa amippa taṟkēṟṟa valivuvāyntu nīṇṭakālam keṭātiruppaṉa vātalāl, avaṟṟāl aracarkaṭku varum varuvāy mikka aḷaviṉatu. mēlpāl uḷḷa cālaṭi nāṭṭil iṟṟikku aiyāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ nākarikattiṟciṟantu viḷaṅkiya aracarkaḷ amitta periya kōyilkaḷum araṇmaṉikaḷum, namatu tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ mēlkarimalikaḷil 
vaḷarā niṉṟa tēkku maraṅkaḷik koṇṭē kaṭṭappaṭṭa pāṉmiyiṉip paḻiya varalāṟṟu nūlāciriyarkaḷ kaṇṭaṟintirukkiṉṟaṉar. immaraṅkaḷēyaṉṟi akil cantaṉam mutalāṉa maṇam kamaḻ maraṅkaḷum, viliyuyarnta yāṉikaḷum, yāṉik kompukaḷ - elumpukaḷum, māṉtōl - pulittōlkaḷum, mayil tōkikaḷum, tēṉ kūṭukaḷum, arakkum, nāviyum, palavakik kāykaṉi kiḻaṅkukaḷ vittukkaḷum kāṭṭiṉ kaṇiruntu nirampap peṟutaliṉ, ivikaḷālum aracarkaḷ mikunta varuvāyi aṭikiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu.
5. marutam - viḷinilam
neṟpayir viḷiyāniṉṟa vayalnilattiyum vayalcūḻnta iṭattiyum marutam eṉṟu tamiḻnūlār paṇṭikkālantoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. paḻiya kālattil neṟ payiriṭutaṟkēṟṟavikaḷākat tiruttappaṭṭa nilaṅkaḷ, maruta maraṅkaḷiṉ arukēyiruntamiyāṟpōlum, avaṟṟiṟku maruta nilam eṉa avar peyar amittaṉar. vayalnilattip paṇṇi, paṇi, cey, ceṟu, kaḻaṉi, paḻaṉam, viḷiyuḷ, pulam, varappuḷ eṉṉum pala coṟkaḷāl tamiḻ makkaḷ vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar.
paḻiya nāṭkaḷil māntariṉ toki perukap peruka, avarkaḷukku vēṇṭiya aḷavu uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷ mali kaḷilum, kaṭalkaḷilum, kāṭukaḷilum kiṭippatu aritāyiṟṟu. malikaḷilum, kāṭukaḷilum uḷḷa kaṉikaḷ, vittukkaḷ, kiḻaṅkukaḷiyum māṉ, mari, kaṭampi, āṭu, māṭu, paṉṟi mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷiyum kaṭalkaḷiluḷḷa mīṉkaḷiyum tiṉṟu vanta mikap paḻiya muṉṉōrkaḷ, tamatu toki perukap peruka avi pōtāmi kaṇṭu, āṭu māṭukaḷi mikutiyāka vaḷarttu avaṟṟiṉ payaṉkaḷāṉa ney, pāl, tayir mutaliyavi kaḷiyum kāṭukaḷil āṅkāṅku iyaṟkiyāka vaḷarntu kiṭanta avari, tuvari, mocci, kārāmaṇi, uḻuntu, payaṟu, kaṭali, eḷḷu mutalāṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷiyum uṭkoṇṭu uyir vāḻntu vantārkaḷ. kālañ cellac cella avarkaḷiṉ toki piṉṉum piṉṉum perukalāṉamiyiṉ, iyaṟkiyil kiṭitta ittaṉi uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷum avarkaṭkup pōtā āyiṉa. itaṟkeṉ ceyvatu eṉṟu, avarkaḷuḷ aṟivāl mikka cilar eṇṇip pārkkap pārkka, iyaṟkiyāka vaḷarum kūlaṅkaḷiṉ vittukkaḷi maṭṭum eṭuttu, vēṟu marañceṭikoṭikaḷ illāta veḷi nilaṅkaḷik kīṟi avaṟṟuḷ vitittāl, avi mikutiyāṉa viḷiccalit tarumeṉak kaṇṭaṟintārkaḷ. aṟintapiṉ aṟiñarkaḷum avaratu ēvalvaḻi niṉṟavarkaḷum veṭṭa veḷiyākakkiṭanta nilaṅkaḷik kōlkaḷālum, kuntālikaḷālum kiḷaṟik kotti avaṟṟiṉkaṇ avvitikaḷi iṭa, avi vēṇṭiya aḷavu veyilum maḻiyum peṟṟu, vēṟu marañceṭi koṭikaḷiṉ iṭiñcal illāmiyiṉ, oṉṟu nūṟākap payaṉtaralāyiṉa.
iṅṅaṉam mutaṉmutal ceyaṟkip payir ceyvataṟku veḷi nilaṅkaḷē payaṉpaṭṭamiyiṉ, avi vayal, pulam eṉṟu peyar peṟalāyiṉa. vayal eṉṉum collukku mutaṟporuḷ veḷiyeṉpatu ākum. pulam eṉṉum collum evi tammālum maṟikkappaṭāmal kaṇṇukkup pulappaṭṭuk kiṭakkum veḷiyiṭattiyē uṇart
tum. mēlum, iyaṟkiyil vaṟitāyk kiṭanta veḷinilam aṟivar cilarāl mutaṉmutal kiṇṭik kiḷaṟip payir ceyvataṟku ēṟṟapaṭiyākac ceyyappaṭṭamiyiṉ piṉṉar atu cey eṉṟum, paṇṇappaṭṭamiyiṉ paṇṇi, paṇi eṉṟum ceṟappaṭṭamiyiṉ atāvatu kīṟappaṭṭami yiṉ ceṟu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭalāyiṟṟu.
ivvāṟu mutalil toṭaṅkiya payirttoḻilic ceyyac ceyya, mēlummēlum ataṉit tiṟamākac ceyyum vaḻi vakikaḷi nam muṉṉōrkaḷ kaṇṭaṟintārkaḷ. valleṉṟu keṭṭiyāyirukkum nilattil meṉpatattavāṉa nellum, ēṉi maṇikaḷum vēr ūṉṟa māṭṭāmal paṭṭup pōtal kaṇṭu vaṉpālāṉa nilatti meṉpālākkum muṟi kaṇṭu koṇṭārkaḷ. nilattik kiḷaṟi ataṟkut taṇṇīr pāycciṉāl maṇṇum taṇṇīrum kalantu atu meṉpatap paṭutal aṟintaṉar. iṅṅaṉam kiḷaṟutalālum taṇṇīr pāyccutalālum iṟukiya maṇ kaḻaṉṟu nilam meṉ patam aṭitaliṉ, aṅṅaṉam kaḻaṉṟa vayalnilam kaḻaṉi eṉappaṭṭatu.
ataṉmēl, payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa akkaḻaṉiyil vittiya nel oṉṟu pallāyiramāy payaṉ tantatākaliṉ piṉṉar atu paḻaṉam, viḷiyuḷ eṉap peyar peṟalāyiṟṟu. payaṉ, paḻam, paḻaṉ, viḷivu mutaliyaṉa ellām orē poruḷit tarum coṟkaḷākum.
